// TYPE     : .TS
// PATH     : src/services/Audio.ts
// PROCESSED: 2026-10-09T19:04:33.964Z
//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE-PATH: src/services/Audio.ts

/**
 * Supported sound effect identifiers within the simulation application.
 * - 'lift': The launching whistle or rocket hiss sound.
 * - 'burst': The primary heavy detonation of a large firework.
 * - 'burstSmall': Secondary or smaller fragment explosions.
 * - 'crackle': Sharp, crackling background ambient sparks.
 * - 'crackleSmall': Rapid, micro-crackle sparkling noise.
 */
export type SoundType = 'lift' | 'burst' | 'burstSmall' | 'crackle' | 'crackleSmall';

/**
 * Internal configuration schema for a specific group of sound assets.
 */
interface AudioConfig {
    // The base volume scalar assigned to this sound group (0.0 to 1.0).
    volume: number;
    // The minimum pitch/playback scale boundary for randomization (e.g., 0.85).
    playbackRateMin: number;
    // The maximum pitch/playback scale boundary for randomization (e.g., 1.15).
    playbackRateMax: number;
    // An array of file name strings representing separate variations of the sound effect.
    readonly fileNames: readonly string[];
    // Caching array housing the fully parsed and decoded in-memory AudioBuffer streams.
    buffers?: AudioBuffer[];
}

/**
 * Configuration options required to instantiate the Audio manager class.
 */
export interface AudioOptions {
    // Dynamic callback function checking if primary audio playback is currently enabled.
    canPlaySound: () => boolean;
    // Dynamic callback evaluating the current speed modifier of the simulation loop.
    getSimulationSpeed: () => number;
    // The root deployment target folder or remote asset CDN url where files reside.
    baseUrl?: string;
}

/**
 * Structural type mapping the cross-browser constructor footprint
 * for the built-in Web Audio Context.
 */
type AudioContextConstructor = { new (contextOptions?: AudioContextOptions): AudioContext; prototype: AudioContext };

/**
 * High-performance audio manager utilizing the Web Audio API.
 *
 * Explicitly designed to handle low-latency playback of dense
 * overlapping sound effects (such as particles and fireworks)
 * by managing audio graphs natively on background browser threads.
 */
export class Audio {
    // The primary hardware execution node container handling memory audio charts.
    private readonly context: AudioContext;
    // Cached application callback validating if global sound playback rules are currently allowed.
    private readonly canPlaySound: () => boolean;
    // Cached application callback fetching real-time simulation speeds to scale playback pitches.
    private readonly getSimulationSpeed: () => number;
    // Sanitized absolute path target location pointing toward the raw static sound assets.
    private readonly baseUrl: string;

    // Internal registry detailing operational volumes, variance structures,
    // and file targets for each effect category.
    private readonly sources: Record<SoundType, AudioConfig> = {
        lift: {
            volume: 1,
            playbackRateMin: 0.85,
            playbackRateMax: 0.95,
            fileNames: ['lift1.mp3', 'lift2.mp3', 'lift3.mp3'],
        },
        burst: {
            volume: 1,
            playbackRateMin: 0.8,
            playbackRateMax: 0.9,
            fileNames: ['burst1.mp3', 'burst2.mp3'],
        },
        burstSmall: {
            volume: 0.25,
            playbackRateMin: 0.8,
            playbackRateMax: 1,
            fileNames: ['burst-sm-1.mp3', 'burst-sm-2.mp3'],
        },
        crackle: {
            volume: 0.2,
            playbackRateMin: 1,
            playbackRateMax: 1,
            fileNames: ['crackle1.mp3'],
        },
        crackleSmall: {
            volume: 0.3,
            playbackRateMin: 1,
            playbackRateMax: 1,
            fileNames: ['crackle-sm-1.mp3'],
        },
    };

    // Epoch timestamp track preventing rapid audio clipping overlaps during
    // heavy particle burst instances.
    private lastSmallBurstTime = 0;

    /**
     * Initializes the Web Audio environment configurations and ensures platform capability.
     * @param options Execution rule settings passed down from the root canvas initialization tree.
     * @throws {Error} If the runtime web browser environment does not support the Web Audio API.
     */
    public constructor(options: AudioOptions) {
        this.canPlaySound = options.canPlaySound;
        this.getSimulationSpeed = options.getSimulationSpeed;

        // Cleanse trailing forward slash symbols out of path strings
        // to prevent file interpolation breaks.
        const providedUrl = options.baseUrl ?? '/sounds/';
        this.baseUrl = providedUrl.endsWith('/') ? providedUrl : `${providedUrl}/`;

        // Resolve vendor prefixes natively mapping to older versions
        // of Apple Webkit engines.
        const AudioContextConstructor: AudioContextConstructor =
            window.AudioContext ??
            (
                window as Window & {
                    webkitAudioContext?: typeof AudioContext;
                }
            ).webkitAudioContext;

        if (!AudioContextConstructor) {
            throw new Error('Web Audio API is not supported in this browser.');
        }

        this.context = new AudioContextConstructor();
    }

    /**
     * Loops through registered configuration profiles to gather, download,
     * and decode audio buffers. Handles dynamic path constructions as basic
     * HTTP fetches to safely avoid build bundle compiler warnings.
     *
     * @returns A promise resolving completely once every single sound configuration group is loaded into memory cache.
     */
    public preload = async (): Promise<void> => {
        const allFilePromises: Promise<AudioBuffer>[] = [];

        for (const source of Object.values(this.sources)) {
            const filePromises: Promise<AudioBuffer>[] = source.fileNames.map(
                (fileName: string): Promise<AudioBuffer> => {
                    const fileUrl: string = `${this.baseUrl}${fileName}`;

                    const promise: Promise<AudioBuffer> = fetch(fileUrl)
                        .then((response: Response): Response => this.checkResponseStatus(response))
                        .then((response: Response): Promise<ArrayBuffer> => response.arrayBuffer())
                        .then((data: ArrayBuffer): Promise<AudioBuffer> => this.decodeAudioData(data));

                    allFilePromises.push(promise);

                    return promise;
                },
            );

            source.buffers = await Promise.all(filePromises);
        }

        await Promise.all(allFilePromises);
    };

    /**
     * Suspends the primary hardware contextual audio thread loop.
     * Instantly mutes all ongoing playback loops and decreases thread
     * processing load when the window minimizes.
     */
    public pauseAll(): void {
        void this.context.suspend();
    }

    /**
     * Wakes the background hardware execution thread stream context up.
     * Triggers a silent mock sound internally to gracefully bypass legacy
     * browser interaction autoplay security rules.
     */
    public resumeAll(): void {
        this.playSound('lift', 0);

        window.setTimeout((): void => {
            void this.context.resume();
        }, 250);
    }

    /**
     * Generates and fires a lightweight standalone hardware streaming
     * source channel loop. Dynamically calculates custom playback rates
     * and scaling nodes based on canvas particle physics sizes.
     *
     * @param type  The sound type profile to trigger.
     * @param scale A multiplier scalar value (0.0 to 1.0) generally
     *              reflecting firework size or proximity depth.
     */
    public playSound(type: SoundType, scale = 1): void {
        const clampedScale: number = this.clamp(scale, 0, 1);

        // Terminate play immediately if blocked by user choices or slow simulation rates.
        if (!this.canPlaySound() || this.getSimulationSpeed() < 0.95) return;

        // Throttling protection: prevents rapid crackles from overloading system speaker hardware.
        if (type === 'burstSmall') {
            const now: number = Date.now();
            if (now - this.lastSmallBurstTime < 20) return;
            this.lastSmallBurstTime = now;
        }

        const source: AudioConfig = this.sources[type];

        if (!source) throw new Error(`Sound of type "${type}" does not exist.`);
        if (!source.buffers?.length) return;

        const initialVolume: number = source.volume;
        const initialPlaybackRate: number = this.random(source.playbackRateMin, source.playbackRateMax);

        // Physics audio scaling mappings (smaller explosions sound lighter and
        // produce slightly snappier high-pitched pop waves).
        const scaledVolume: number = initialVolume * clampedScale;
        const scaledPlaybackRate: number = initialPlaybackRate * (2 - clampedScale);

        // Hardware Routing: Setup a programmable Gain control processor
        //  module inside the Context scope.
        const gainNode = this.context.createGain();
        gainNode.gain.value = scaledVolume;

        const buffer: AudioBuffer = this.randomChoice(source.buffers);

        // Hardware Routing: Instantiate an ultra-fast hardware pointer tracking
        // reference link node.
        const bufferSource: AudioBufferSourceNode = this.context.createBufferSource();
        bufferSource.playbackRate.value = scaledPlaybackRate;
        bufferSource.buffer = buffer;

        // Chain components:
        // [Raw Streaming Data Node] --->
        //                         [Volume Gain Modifier Module] --->
        //                                                     [System Main Output Speakers]
        bufferSource.connect(gainNode);
        gainNode.connect(this.context.destination);

        // Deploy instantly into action (Zero seconds time-delay target).
        bufferSource.start(0);
    }

    /**
     * Decodes the raw binary array data into an AudioBuffer using the modern Promise API.
     * Fixes signature incompatibility errors introduced by strict compiler engine checks.
     *
     * @param data Raw compressed asset array chunks pulled downstream from network fetches.
     * @returns A promise resolving to a decoded hardware-built-in operational clip array.
     */
    private async decodeAudioData(data: ArrayBuffer): Promise<AudioBuffer> {
        return this.context.decodeAudioData(data);
    }

    /**
     * Validates if HTTP operations returned standard success status flags.
     *
     * @param response The network response stream incoming from asset directories.
     * @returns The safe upstream verified response mapping configuration.
     * @throws {Error} If resource targets are missing (404) or blocked by CORS issues.
     */
    private checkResponseStatus(response: Response): Response {
        if (response.ok) return response;
        throw new Error(`Unable to load audio asset: ${response.status} ${response.statusText}`);
    }

    /**
     * Utility boundaries lock module keeping numbers comfortably
     * pinned between a minimum and maximum range.
     *
     * @param value Target metric evaluating variables.
     * @param min Lowest allowed constraint ceiling.
     * @param max Highest allowed constraint ceiling.
     * @returns The safely clamped final position outcome.
     */
    private clamp(value: number, min: number, max: number): number {
        return Math.min(Math.max(value, min), max);
    }

    /**
     * Generates a pseudorandom floating-point value.
     * Used to vary sound effects slightly so repetitive firework
     * sounds don't sound mechanical or artificial.
     *
     * @param min Lower scope bounding value.
     * @param max Upper scope bounding value.
     * @returns A randomized entry matching the calculated layout bounds.
     */
    private random(min: number, max: number): number {
        return Math.random() * (max - min) + min;
    }

    /**
     * Picks an item completely at random from a given sequence list.
     *
     * @param items Shared generic collection array targets.
     * @returns A singular random array item selected from the collection.
     */
    private randomChoice<T>(items: readonly T[]): T {
        const index: number = Math.floor(Math.random() * items.length);
        return items[index]!;
    }
}

//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-<  END  >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■

//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE NAME: : FullScreen.ts 
// TYPE     : .TS
// PATH     : src/services/FullScreen.ts
// PROCESSED: 2026-10-09T19:04:33.964Z
//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE-PATH: src/services/FullScreen.ts

// oxlint-disable typescript/no-explicit-any

type EventHandler = (this: FScreen, ev: Event) => any;
type EventHandlerOptions = AddEventListenerOptions | boolean | undefined;
type FullscreenEvent = 'fullscreenchange' | 'fullscreenerror' | '';

interface FScreen {
    requestFullscreen: (element: Element) => Promise<void> | void;
    requestFullscreenFunction: (element: Element) => Promise<void> | void;
    readonly exitFullscreen: () => Promise<void> | void;
    addEventListener: (type: FullscreenEvent, handler: EventHandler, options?: EventHandlerOptions) => void;
    removeEventListener: (type: FullscreenEvent, handler: EventHandler) => void;
    fullscreenEnabled: boolean;
    fullscreenElement: Element | null;
    onfullscreenchange: EventHandler | null;
    onfullscreenerror: EventHandler | null;
}

const KeyNames = [
    'fullscreenEnabled',
    'fullscreenElement',
    'requestFullscreen',
    'exitFullscreen',
    'fullscreenchange',
    'fullscreenerror',
] as const;

type KeyName = typeof KeyNames;
type Keys = keyof typeof KeyNames;

type KeyRegistry = {
    [I in Keys as KeyName[I] extends string ? KeyName[I] : never]: I extends `${infer N extends number}` ? N : never;
};

const key: KeyRegistry = {
    fullscreenEnabled: 0,
    fullscreenElement: 1,
    requestFullscreen: 2,
    exitFullscreen: 3,
    fullscreenchange: 4,
    fullscreenerror: 5,
} as const;

const webkit: string[] = [
    'webkitFullscreenEnabled',
    'webkitFullscreenElement',
    'webkitRequestFullscreen',
    'webkitExitFullscreen',
    'webkitfullscreenchange',
    'webkitfullscreenerror',
] as const;

const moz: string[] = [
    'mozFullScreenEnabled',
    'mozFullScreenElement',
    'mozRequestFullScreen',
    'mozCancelFullScreen',
    'mozfullscreenchange',
    'mozfullscreenerror',
] as const;

const ms: string[] = [
    'msFullscreenEnabled',
    'msFullscreenElement',
    'msRequestFullscreen',
    'msExitFullscreen',
    'MSFullscreenChange',
    'MSFullscreenError',
] as const;

const doc: any = typeof window?.document !== 'undefined' ? window.document : ({} as const);

const vendor: string[] =
    ('fullscreenEnabled' in doc && Object.keys(key)) ||
    (webkit[0] in doc && webkit) ||
    (moz[0] in doc && moz) ||
    (ms[0] in doc && ms) ||
    ([] as const);

const FSCREEN = {
    requestFullscreen: (element: Element): Promise<void> | void => {
        const method: string = vendor[key.requestFullscreen];
        return (element as any)[method]?.();
    },
    requestFullscreenFunction: (element: Element): Promise<void> | void => {
        return (element as any)[vendor[key.requestFullscreen]];
    },
    addEventListener: (type: string, handler: EventHandler, options?: EventHandlerOptions): void => {
        return doc.addEventListener?.(vendor[key[type]], handler, options);
    },
    removeEventListener: (type: string, handler: EventHandler): void => {
        return doc.removeEventListener?.(vendor[key[type]], handler);
    },
    get exitFullscreen(): () => Promise<void> | void {
        const method: string = vendor[key.exitFullscreen];
        return doc[method] ? doc[method].bind(doc) : (): void => {};
    },
    get fullscreenEnabled(): boolean {
        return Boolean(doc[vendor[key.fullscreenEnabled]]);
    },
    set fullscreenEnabled(_val: boolean) {},
    get fullscreenElement(): Element | null {
        return doc[vendor[key.fullscreenElement]] ?? null;
    },
    set fullscreenElement(_val: Element | null) {},
    get onfullscreenchange(): EventHandler | null {
        const vendorKey: string = vendor[key.fullscreenchange];
        return vendorKey ? doc[('on' + vendorKey).toLowerCase()] : null;
    },
    set onfullscreenchange(handler: EventHandler | null) {
        const vendorKey: string = vendor[key.fullscreenchange];
        if (vendorKey) doc[('on' + vendorKey).toLowerCase()] = handler;
    },
    get onfullscreenerror(): EventHandler | null {
        const vendorKey: string = vendor[key.fullscreenerror];
        return vendorKey ? doc[('on' + vendorKey).toLowerCase()] : null;
    },
    set onfullscreenerror(handler: EventHandler | null) {
        const vendorKey: string = vendor[key.fullscreenerror];
        if (vendorKey) doc[('on' + vendorKey).toLowerCase()] = handler;
    },
} as const;

export type FullScreen = typeof FSCREEN;

export interface FullscreenControllerOptions {
    readonly target: Element;

    onChange(isFullscreen: boolean): void;
}

export class FullscreenController {
    readonly fullscreen: FullScreen;
    private readonly options: FullscreenControllerOptions;

    public constructor(options: FullscreenControllerOptions) {
        this.options = options;
        this.fullscreen = FSCREEN;
    }

    // Fullscreen helpers, using Fscreen for prefixes.
    public get isSupported(): boolean {
        return this.fullscreen.fullscreenEnabled;
    }

    // Note that fullscreen state is synced to store, and the store should be the source
    // of truth for whether the app is in fullscreen mode or not.
    public get isActive(): boolean {
        return this.fullscreen.fullscreenElement !== null;
    }

    public mount(): void {
        this.fullscreen.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', this.handleFullscreenChange);
    }

    public destroy(): void {
        this.fullscreen.removeEventListener('fullscreenchange', this.handleFullscreenChange);
    }

    // Attempt to toggle fullscreen mode.
    public async toggle(): Promise<void> {
        if (!this.isSupported) {
            return;
        }

        if (this.isActive) {
            await this.fullscreen.exitFullscreen();
            return;
        }

        await this.fullscreen.requestFullscreen(this.options.target);
    }

    // Sync fullscreen changes with store. An event listener is necessary because the user can
    // toggle fullscreen mode directly through the browser, and we want to react to that.
    private readonly handleFullscreenChange: () => void = (): void => {
        this.options.onChange(this.isActive);
    };
}

//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■

// PROCESSED: 2026-10-09T19:04:33.964Z

// FILE-PATH: src/services/Storage.ts

//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-<  END  >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■

//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE NAME: : CanvasRenderer.ts 
// TYPE     : .TS
// PATH     : src/engine/CanvasRenderer.ts
// PROCESSED: 2026-10-09T19:04:33.964Z
//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE-PATH: src/engine/CanvasRenderer.ts

import type { Stage } from './Stage';
import type { BurstFlashManager, BurstFlashParticle } from './particles/BurstFlash';
import type { SparkManager, SparkParticle } from './particles/Spark';
import type { StarManager, StarParticle } from './particles/Star';
import { type VisibleColor, BURSTCOLOR, COLOR, COLOR_CODES, COLOR_TUPLES, RGBColor } from '../constants/colors';
import { type SkylightLevel, SKYLIGHT, toSkylightLevel } from '../constants/config';

export interface CanvasRendererOptions {
    readonly trailsStage: Stage;
    readonly mainStage: Stage;

    readonly stars: StarManager;
    readonly sparks: SparkManager;
    readonly burstFlashes: BurstFlashManager;

    readonly canvasContainer: HTMLElement;

    getLogicalWidth(): number;
    getLogicalHeight(): number;

    getScaleFactor(): number;
    isLongExposureEnabled(): boolean;

    getSkyLightingLevel(): SkylightLevel;

    getSimulationSpeed(): number;
    getSpeedBarOpacity(): number;
    isLowQuality(): boolean;
}

export class CanvasRenderer {
    private readonly options: CanvasRendererOptions;
    private currentSkyColor = new RGBColor();
    private targetSkyColor = new RGBColor();

    public constructor(options: CanvasRendererOptions) {
        this.options = options;
    }

    private clear(speed: number): void {
        const width: number = this.options.getLogicalWidth();
        const height: number = this.options.getLogicalHeight();

        // Convert current sky colors to clean integer values
        const skyR: number = this.currentSkyColor.r | 0;
        const skyG: number = this.currentSkyColor.g | 0;
        const skyB: number = this.currentSkyColor.b | 0;

        // Choose opacity blend based on long exposure selection
        const alpha: number = this.options.isLongExposureEnabled() ? 0.0025 : 0.175 * speed;

        // Clear and fade the trails using the dynamic sky color on the CANVAS instead of the DOM
        this.options.trailsStage.ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(${skyR}, ${skyG}, ${skyB}, ${alpha})`;
        this.options.trailsStage.ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

        this.options.mainStage.ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);
    }

    public render(speed: number): void {
        const dpr: number = this.options.mainStage.dpr;
        const width: number = this.options.getLogicalWidth();
        const height: number = this.options.getLogicalHeight();

        const trailsCtx: CanvasRenderingContext2D = this.options.trailsStage.ctx;
        const mainCtx: CanvasRenderingContext2D = this.options.mainStage.ctx;

        if (this.options.getSkyLightingLevel() !== toSkylightLevel(SKYLIGHT.NONE)) {
            this.colorSky(speed);
        }

        // Cache parameters locally to avoid multiple cross-boundary object reads
        const scaleFactor: number = this.options.getScaleFactor();
        const finalScale: number = dpr * scaleFactor;
        const isLowQuality: boolean = this.options.isLowQuality();

        // Reset identity Matrix BEFORE setting the scaling factor
        // This eliminates exponential coordinate amplification
        trailsCtx.setTransform(finalScale, 0, 0, finalScale, 0, 0);
        mainCtx.setTransform(finalScale, 0, 0, finalScale, 0, 0);

        trailsCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';

        this.clear(speed);

        /**
         ** Draw queued burst flashes
         */
        const bfActive: BurstFlashParticle[] = this.options.burstFlashes.active;
        while (bfActive.length > 0) {
            const bf: BurstFlashParticle = bfActive.pop()!;
            const bx: number = bf.x | 0;
            const by: number = bf.y | 0;
            const br: number = bf.radius;

            const burstGradient: CanvasGradient = trailsCtx.createRadialGradient(bx, by, 0, bx, by, br);

            burstGradient.addColorStop(0.024, BURSTCOLOR.Burst);
            burstGradient.addColorStop(0.125, BURSTCOLOR.Glow);
            burstGradient.addColorStop(0.32, BURSTCOLOR.Trail);
            burstGradient.addColorStop(1, BURSTCOLOR.Exhaust);

            trailsCtx.fillStyle = burstGradient;
            // Draw matching integer coordinate frames to prevent layout bleeding
            trailsCtx.fillRect(bx - br, by - br, br * 2, br * 2);

            this.options.burstFlashes.returnInstance(bf);
        }

        trailsCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighten';

        /**
         ** Draw stars (Optimized Loop Structure)
         */
        trailsCtx.lineWidth = this.options.stars.drawWidth;
        trailsCtx.lineCap = isLowQuality ? 'square' : 'round';
        mainCtx.strokeStyle = COLOR.White;
        mainCtx.lineWidth = 1;

        mainCtx.beginPath();

        const colorsLen: number = COLOR_CODES.length;
        for (let c: number = 0; c < colorsLen; c++) {
            const color: VisibleColor = COLOR_CODES[c];
            const stars: StarParticle[] = this.options.stars.active[color];
            const starsLen: number = stars.length;

            trailsCtx.strokeStyle = color;
            trailsCtx.beginPath();

            for (let s: number = 0; s < starsLen; s++) {
                const star: StarParticle = stars[s];
                if (star.visible) {
                    // Whole number coordinates (| 0) prevent slow anti-aliasing math
                    const sx: number = star.x | 0;
                    const sy: number = star.y | 0;

                    trailsCtx.moveTo(sx, sy);
                    trailsCtx.lineTo(star.prevX | 0, star.prevY | 0);

                    mainCtx.moveTo(sx, sy);
                    mainCtx.lineTo((star.x - star.speedX * 1.6) | 0, (star.y - star.speedY * 1.6) | 0);
                }
            }
            trailsCtx.stroke();
        }
        mainCtx.stroke();

        /**
         ** Draw sparks (Optimized Loop Structure)
         */
        trailsCtx.lineWidth = this.options.sparks.drawWidth;
        trailsCtx.lineCap = 'butt';

        for (let c: number = 0; c < colorsLen; c++) {
            const color: VisibleColor = COLOR_CODES[c];
            const sparks: SparkParticle[] = this.options.sparks.active[color];
            const sparksLen: number = sparks.length;

            trailsCtx.strokeStyle = color;
            trailsCtx.beginPath();

            for (let sp: number = 0; sp < sparksLen; sp++) {
                const spark: SparkParticle = sparks[sp];
                trailsCtx.moveTo(spark.x | 0, spark.y | 0);
                trailsCtx.lineTo(spark.prevX | 0, spark.prevY | 0);
            }
            trailsCtx.stroke();
        }

        /**
         ** Render speed bar if visible
         */
        const barOpacity: number = this.options.getSpeedBarOpacity();
        if (barOpacity > 0) {
            const speedBarHeight = 6;
            mainCtx.globalAlpha = barOpacity;
            mainCtx.fillStyle = COLOR.Blue;
            mainCtx.fillRect(
                0,
                (height - speedBarHeight) | 0,
                (width * this.options.getSimulationSpeed()) | 0,
                speedBarHeight,
            );
            mainCtx.globalAlpha = 1;
        }

        // Return clean, unscaled space for container bindings
        trailsCtx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
        mainCtx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
    }

    // Draw colored overlay based on combined brightness of stars (light up the sky!)
    // Note: this is applied to the canvas container's background, so it's behind the particles
    private colorSky(speed: number): void {
        // The maximum r, g, or b value that will be used (255 would represent no maximum)
        const maxSkySaturation: number = Number(this.options.getSkyLightingLevel()) * 15;

        // How many stars are required in total to reach maximum sky brightness
        const maxStarCount: number = 500;
        let totalStarCount: number = 0;

        // Initialize sky as black
        this.targetSkyColor.r = 0;
        this.targetSkyColor.g = 0;
        this.targetSkyColor.b = 0;

        // Add each known color to sky, multiplied by particle count of that color. This will put
        // RGB values wildly out of bounds, but we'll scale them back later.
        // Also add up total star count.
        const colorsLen: number = COLOR_CODES.length;
        for (let c: number = 0; c < colorsLen; c++) {
            const color: VisibleColor = COLOR_CODES[c];
            const tuple: RGBColor = COLOR_TUPLES[color];
            const count: number = this.options.stars.active[color].length;

            totalStarCount += count;
            this.targetSkyColor.r += tuple.r * count;
            this.targetSkyColor.g += tuple.g * count;
            this.targetSkyColor.b += tuple.b * count;
        }

        // Clamp intensity at 1.0, and map to a custom non-linear curve. This allows few stars to
        // perceivably light up the sky, while more stars continue to increase the brightness but
        // at a lesser rate. This is more inline with humans' non-linear brightness perception.
        const intensity: number = Math.pow(Math.min(1, totalStarCount / maxStarCount), 0.3);

        // Figure out which color component has the highest value, so we can scale them without
        // affecting the ratios.
        // Prevent 0 from being used, so we don't divide by zero in the next step.
        const maxColor: number = Math.max(1, this.targetSkyColor.r, this.targetSkyColor.g, this.targetSkyColor.b);

        // Scale all color components to a max of `maxSkySaturation`, and apply intensity.
        this.targetSkyColor.r = (this.targetSkyColor.r / maxColor) * maxSkySaturation * intensity;
        this.targetSkyColor.g = (this.targetSkyColor.g / maxColor) * maxSkySaturation * intensity;
        this.targetSkyColor.b = (this.targetSkyColor.b / maxColor) * maxSkySaturation * intensity;

        // Animate changes to color to smooth out transitions.
        const colorChange: number = 10;
        this.currentSkyColor.r += ((this.targetSkyColor.r - this.currentSkyColor.r) / colorChange) * speed;
        this.currentSkyColor.g += ((this.targetSkyColor.g - this.currentSkyColor.g) / colorChange) * speed;
        this.currentSkyColor.b += ((this.targetSkyColor.b - this.currentSkyColor.b) / colorChange) * speed;

        // const r: number = this.currentSkyColor.r | 0;
        // const g: number = this.currentSkyColor.g | 0;
        // const b: number = this.currentSkyColor.b | 0;
        // this.options.canvasContainer.style.backgroundColor = `rgb(${r}, ${g}, ${b})`;
    }
}

// PROCESSED: 2026-10-09T19:04:33.964Z
//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE-PATH: src/engine/ColorPicker.ts

import { COLOR, VISIBLE_COLORS, type VisibleColor } from '../constants/colors';

interface RandomColorOptions {
    notSame?: boolean;
    notColor?: VisibleColor;
    limitWhite?: boolean;
}

class ColorPicker {
    private lastColor: VisibleColor | undefined;

    // Get a random color, with some customization options available.
    public random(options: RandomColorOptions = {}): VisibleColor {
        let notSame: boolean = options.notSame ?? false;
        let notColor: VisibleColor | undefined = options.notColor;
        let limitWhite: boolean = options.limitWhite ?? false;

        let color: VisibleColor = this.randomSimple();

        // limit the amount of white chosen randomly
        if (limitWhite && color === COLOR.White && Math.random() < 0.6) {
            color = this.randomSimple();
        }

        if (notSame) {
            while (color === this.lastColor) {
                color = this.randomSimple();
            }
        } else if (notColor) {
            while (color === notColor) {
                color = this.randomSimple();
            }
        }

        this.lastColor = color;
        return color;
    }

    // Get a random color.
    private randomSimple(): VisibleColor {
        const index: number = Math.floor(Math.random() * VISIBLE_COLORS.length);
        return VISIBLE_COLORS[index]!;
    }

    public whiteOrGold(): VisibleColor {
        return Math.random() < 0.5 ? COLOR.Gold : COLOR.White;
    }

    public makePistilColor(shellColor: VisibleColor): VisibleColor {
        return shellColor === COLOR.White || shellColor === COLOR.Gold
            ? this.random({ notColor: shellColor })
            : this.whiteOrGold();
    }
}

export { type RandomColorOptions, ColorPicker };

// FILE-PATH: src/engine/Engine.ts

import { Stage, type PointerEventPayload } from './Stage';
import type { TickerEvent } from './Ticker';
import type { AppNodes } from '../dom/AppNodes';
import type { AppConfig } from '../app/AppState';

export interface EngineCallbacks {
    isRunning(): boolean;
    getConfig(): Readonly<AppConfig>;
    onPointerStart(event: PointerEventPayload): void;
    onPointerEnd(event: PointerEventPayload): void;
    onPointerMove(event: PointerEventPayload): void;
    onTicker(event: TickerEvent): void;
    onResize(width: number, height: number): void;
}

export interface EngineOptions {
    readonly nodes: AppNodes;
    readonly callbacks: EngineCallbacks;
    readonly maxWidth: number;
    readonly maxHeight: number;
}

export class Engine {
    public readonly trailsStage: Stage;
    public readonly mainStage: Stage;
    public readonly stages: readonly Stage[];
    private readonly options: EngineOptions;

    public constructor(options: EngineOptions) {
        this.options = options;

        this.trailsStage = new Stage(this.options.nodes.trailsCanvas, 'webgl2');
        this.mainStage = new Stage(this.options.nodes.mainCanvas, '2d');
        this.stages = [this.trailsStage, this.mainStage];
    }

    public initialize(): void {
        this.mainStage.addEventListener('pointerstart', this.handlePointerStart);
        this.mainStage.addEventListener('pointerend', this.handlePointerEnd);
        this.mainStage.addEventListener('pointermove', this.handlePointerMove);
        this.mainStage.addEventListener('ticker', this.handleTicker);

        window.addEventListener('resize', this.handleWindowResize);
        this.resize();
    }

    public resize(): void {
        const windowWidth: number = window.innerWidth;
        const windowHeight: number = window.innerHeight;
        const containerWidth: number = Math.min(windowWidth, this.options.maxWidth);
        const containerHeight: number =
            windowWidth <= 420 ? windowHeight : Math.min(windowHeight, this.options.maxHeight);

        this.options.nodes.stageContainer.style.width = `${containerWidth}px`;
        this.options.nodes.stageContainer.style.height = `${containerHeight}px`;

        for (const stage of this.stages) {
            stage.resize(containerWidth, containerHeight);
        }

        // FOR DEBUGGING:
        //
        // const trailsRect: DOMRect = this.trailsStage.canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
        // const mainRect: DOMRect = this.mainStage.canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
        //
        // console.table([
        //     {
        //         canvas: 'trails',
        //         stageWidth: this.trailsStage.width,
        //         stageHeight: this.trailsStage.height,
        //         cssWidth: trailsRect.width,
        //         cssHeight: trailsRect.height,
        //         inlineWidth: this.trailsStage.canvas.style.width,
        //         inlineHeight: this.trailsStage.canvas.style.height,
        //     },
        //     {
        //         canvas: 'main',
        //         stageWidth: this.mainStage.width,
        //         stageHeight: this.mainStage.height,
        //         cssWidth: mainRect.width,
        //         cssHeight: mainRect.height,
        //         inlineWidth: this.mainStage.canvas.style.width,
        //         inlineHeight: this.mainStage.canvas.style.height,
        //     },
        // ]);

        this.options.callbacks.onResize(containerWidth, containerHeight);
    }

    public destroy(): void {
        this.mainStage.removeEventListener('pointerstart', this.handlePointerStart);
        this.mainStage.removeEventListener('pointerend', this.handlePointerEnd);
        this.mainStage.removeEventListener('pointermove', this.handlePointerMove);
        this.mainStage.removeEventListener('ticker', this.handleTicker);
        window.removeEventListener('resize', this.handleWindowResize);
    }

    private readonly handleWindowResize: (event: UIEvent) => void = (_event: UIEvent): void => {
        this.resize();
    };

    private readonly handlePointerStart: (event: PointerEventPayload) => void = (event: PointerEventPayload): void => {
        this.options.callbacks.onPointerStart(event);
    };

    private readonly handlePointerEnd: (event: PointerEventPayload) => void = (event: PointerEventPayload): void => {
        this.options.callbacks.onPointerEnd(event);
    };

    private readonly handlePointerMove: (event: PointerEventPayload) => void = (event: PointerEventPayload): void => {
        this.options.callbacks.onPointerMove(event);
    };

    private readonly handleTicker: (event: TickerEvent) => void = (event: TickerEvent): void => {
        this.options.callbacks.onTicker(event);
    };
}

// FILE-PATH: src/engine/FireworksRuntime.ts

import type { AppConfig } from '../app/AppState';
import type { AppNodes } from '../dom/AppNodes';
import type { ParticleColor } from '../constants/colors';
import type { SkylightLevel } from '../constants/config';
import type { Audio } from '../services/Audio';

import type { BurstFlashManager } from './particles/BurstFlash';
import type { SparkManager } from './particles/Spark';
import type { StarManager } from './particles/Star';
import type { ConfiguredShellName } from './ShellTypes';
import type { TickerEvent } from './Ticker';
import type { PointerEventPayload } from './Stage';

import { WebGLRenderer } from './WebGLRenderer';
import { Engine } from './Engine';
import { FireworksSimulation } from './FireworksSimulation';
import { InteractionController } from './InteractionController';
import { ParticleSimulation } from './ParticleSimulation';
import { ShellSystem } from './ShellSystem';
import type { PerformancePane } from '../debug/PerformancePane';

export interface FireworksRuntimeOptions {
    readonly nodes: AppNodes;

    readonly stars: StarManager;
    readonly sparks: SparkManager;
    readonly burstFlashes: BurstFlashManager;
    readonly audio: Audio;
    readonly performancePane: PerformancePane;

    readonly particleColors: readonly ParticleColor[];
    readonly gravity: number;

    readonly maxWidth: number;
    readonly maxHeight: number;

    readonly isDesktopMode: boolean;
    readonly isHeaderMode: boolean;

    isRunning(): boolean;
    getConfig(): Readonly<AppConfig>;

    togglePause(): void;
    toggleSound(): void;
    toggleMenu(open?: boolean): void;

    getScaleFactor(): number;
    isLongExposureEnabled(): boolean;
    getSkyLightingLevel(): SkylightLevel;

    getQuality(): number;
    isLowQuality(): boolean;
    isHighQuality(): boolean;

    getSelectedShellSize(): number;
    getConfiguredShellName(): ConfiguredShellName;
    isFinaleEnabled(): boolean;
}

export class FireworksRuntime {
    public readonly engine: Engine;
    public readonly shellSystem: ShellSystem;
    public readonly interactionController: InteractionController;
    public readonly webGLRenderer: WebGLRenderer;
    public readonly particleSimulation: ParticleSimulation;
    public readonly fireworksSimulation: FireworksSimulation;

    private readonly options: FireworksRuntimeOptions;

    private logicalWidth: number = 0;
    private logicalHeight: number = 0;
    private initialized: boolean = false;

    private fireworksSimulationReference: FireworksSimulation | null = null;

    public constructor(options: FireworksRuntimeOptions) {
        this.options = options;

        this.engine = new Engine({
            nodes: this.options.nodes,
            maxWidth: this.options.maxWidth,
            maxHeight: this.options.maxHeight,
            callbacks: {
                isRunning: (): boolean => this.options.isRunning(),
                getConfig: (): Readonly<AppConfig> => this.options.getConfig(),
                onPointerStart: (event: PointerEventPayload): void =>
                    this.interactionController.handlePointerStart(event),
                onPointerEnd: (event: PointerEventPayload): void => this.interactionController.handlePointerEnd(event),
                onPointerMove: (event: PointerEventPayload): void =>
                    this.interactionController.handlePointerMove(event),
                onTicker: (event: TickerEvent): void => this.getFireworksSimulation().update(event),
                onResize: (containerWidth: number, containerHeight: number): void =>
                    this.updateLogicalDimensions(containerWidth, containerHeight),
            },
        });

        this.shellSystem = new ShellSystem({
            stars: this.options.stars,
            sparks: this.options.sparks,
            burstFlashes: this.options.burstFlashes,
            audio: this.options.audio,
            isDesktopMode: this.options.isDesktopMode,
            isHeaderMode: this.options.isHeaderMode,
            getStageWidth: (): number => this.logicalWidth,
            getStageHeight: (): number => this.logicalHeight,
            getCanvasWidth: (): number => this.engine.mainStage.width,
            getCanvasHeight: (): number => this.engine.mainStage.height,
            getQuality: (): number => this.options.getQuality(),
            isHighQuality: (): boolean => this.options.isHighQuality(),
            getSelectedShellSize: (): number => this.options.getSelectedShellSize(),
            getConfiguredShellName: (): ConfiguredShellName => this.options.getConfiguredShellName(),
            isFinaleEnabled: (): boolean => this.options.isFinaleEnabled(),
        });

        this.interactionController = new InteractionController({
            getStageWidth: (): number => this.engine.mainStage.width,
            getStageHeight: (): number => this.engine.mainStage.height,
            isRunning: (): boolean => this.options.isRunning(),
            togglePause: (): void => this.options.togglePause(),
            toggleSound: (): void => this.options.toggleSound(),
            toggleMenu: (open?: boolean): void => this.options.toggleMenu(open),
            launchShell: (event: PointerEventPayload): void => this.shellSystem.launcher.launchFromPointer(event),
            getSimulationSpeed: (): number => this.getFireworksSimulation().getSimulationSpeed(),
            setSimulationSpeed: (speed: number): void => this.getFireworksSimulation().setSimulationSpeed(speed),
            showSpeedBar: (): void => this.getFireworksSimulation().showSpeedBar(),
        });

        this.webGLRenderer = new WebGLRenderer({
            trailsStage: this.engine.trailsStage,
            mainStage: this.engine.mainStage,
            stars: this.options.stars,
            sparks: this.options.sparks,
            burstFlashes: this.options.burstFlashes,
            canvasContainer: this.options.nodes.canvasContainer,
            getLogicalWidth: (): number => this.logicalWidth,
            getLogicalHeight: (): number => this.logicalHeight,
            getScaleFactor: (): number => this.options.getScaleFactor(),
            isLongExposureEnabled: (): boolean => this.options.isLongExposureEnabled(),
            getSkyLightingLevel: (): SkylightLevel => this.options.getSkyLightingLevel(),
            getSimulationSpeed: (): number => this.getFireworksSimulation().getSimulationSpeed(),
            getSpeedBarOpacity: (): number => this.getFireworksSimulation().getSpeedBarOpacity(),
            isLowQuality: (): boolean => this.options.isLowQuality(),
        });

        this.particleSimulation = new ParticleSimulation({
            stars: this.options.stars,
            sparks: this.options.sparks,
            particleColors: this.options.particleColors,
            gravity: this.options.gravity,
            isLowQuality: (): boolean => this.options.isLowQuality(),
        });

        this.fireworksSimulation = new FireworksSimulation({
            particleSimulation: this.particleSimulation,
            webGLRenderer: this.webGLRenderer,
            sequenceController: this.shellSystem.sequences,
            isRunning: (): boolean => this.options.isRunning(),
            isAutoLaunchEnabled: (): boolean => this.options.getConfig().autoLaunch,
            isSpeedAdjusting: (): boolean => this.interactionController.isUpdatingSpeed,
            beginPerformanceFrame: (): void => this.options.performancePane.begin(),
            endPerformanceFrame: (): void => this.options.performancePane.end(),
        });

        this.fireworksSimulationReference = this.fireworksSimulation;
    }

    public initialize(): void {
        if (this.initialized) return;
        this.initialized = true;
        window.addEventListener('keydown', this.handleKeydown);
        this.engine.initialize();
    }

    public destroy(): void {
        if (!this.initialized) return;
        this.initialized = false;
        window.removeEventListener('keydown', this.handleKeydown);
        this.engine.destroy();
    }

    public resize(): void {
        this.engine.resize();
    }

    private getFireworksSimulation(): FireworksSimulation {
        if (this.fireworksSimulationReference === null) {
            throw new Error('FireworksSimulation is unavailable before initialization.');
        }
        return this.fireworksSimulationReference;
    }

    private updateLogicalDimensions(containerWidth: number, containerHeight: number): void {
        const scaleFactor: number = this.options.getScaleFactor();
        this.logicalWidth = containerWidth / scaleFactor;
        this.logicalHeight = containerHeight / scaleFactor;
    }

    private readonly handleKeydown: (event: KeyboardEvent) => void = (event: KeyboardEvent): void => {
        this.interactionController.handleKeydown(event);
    };
}

// PROCESSED: 2026-10-09T19:04:33.965Z
//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE-PATH: src/engine/FireworksSimulation.ts

import type { TickerEvent } from './Ticker';
import type { WebGLRenderer } from './WebGLRenderer';
import type { ParticleSimulation } from './ParticleSimulation';
import type { SequenceController } from './SequenceController';

export interface FireworksSimulationOptions {
    readonly particleSimulation: ParticleSimulation;
    readonly webGLRenderer: WebGLRenderer;
    readonly sequenceController: SequenceController;

    isRunning(): boolean;
    isAutoLaunchEnabled(): boolean;
    isSpeedAdjusting(): boolean;
    beginPerformanceFrame(): void;
    endPerformanceFrame(): void;
}

export class FireworksSimulation {
    private readonly options: FireworksSimulationOptions;

    private simulationSpeed: number = 1;
    private speedBarOpacity: number = 0;
    private autoLaunchTime: number = 0;

    public constructor(options: FireworksSimulationOptions) {
        this.options = options;
    }

    public getSimulationSpeed(): number {
        return this.simulationSpeed;
    }

    public setSimulationSpeed(speed: number): void {
        this.simulationSpeed = Math.min(Math.max(speed, 0), 1);
    }

    public getSpeedBarOpacity(): number {
        return this.speedBarOpacity;
    }

    public showSpeedBar(): void {
        this.speedBarOpacity = 1;
    }

    public update(tickerEvent: TickerEvent): void {
        if (!this.options.isRunning()) {
            return;
        }

        this.options.beginPerformanceFrame();

        try {
            const frameTime: number = tickerEvent.frameTime;
            const lag: number = tickerEvent.lag;

            if (!Number.isFinite(frameTime) || !Number.isFinite(lag)) {
                throw new Error(`Invalid ticker values: frameTime=${String(frameTime)}, lag=${String(lag)}`);
            }

            const timeStep: number = frameTime * this.simulationSpeed;
            const speed: number = this.simulationSpeed * lag;

            this.updateRuntimeState(timeStep, lag);

            // Particle physics and effects
            this.options.particleSimulation.update(timeStep, speed);

            // Canvas rendering
            this.options.webGLRenderer.render(speed);
        } finally {
            this.options.endPerformanceFrame();
        }
    }

    private updateRuntimeState(timeStep: number, lag: number): void {
        if (!this.options.isSpeedAdjusting()) {
            this.speedBarOpacity -= lag / 30;

            if (this.speedBarOpacity < 0) {
                this.speedBarOpacity = 0;
            }
        }

        if (this.options.isAutoLaunchEnabled()) {
            this.autoLaunchTime -= timeStep;

            if (this.autoLaunchTime <= 0) {
                this.autoLaunchTime = this.options.sequenceController.startNextSequence() * 1.25;
            }
        }
    }
}

//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE NAME: : InteractionController.ts 
// TYPE     : .TS
// PATH     : src/engine/InteractionController.ts
// PROCESSED: 2026-10-09T19:04:33.965Z
//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE-PATH: src/engine/InteractionController.ts

import type { PointerEventPayload } from '../utils/Stage';

export interface InteractionControllerOptions {
    getStageWidth(): number;
    getStageHeight(): number;

    isRunning(): boolean;

    togglePause(): void;
    toggleSound(): void;
    toggleMenu(open?: boolean): void;

    launchShell(event: PointerEventPayload): void;

    getSimulationSpeed(): number;
    setSimulationSpeed(speed: number): void;

    showSpeedBar(): void;
}

export class InteractionController {
    private readonly options: InteractionControllerOptions;

    private activePointerCount: number = 0;
    public isUpdatingSpeed: boolean = false;

    public constructor(options: InteractionControllerOptions) {
        this.options = options;
    }

    public handlePointerStart(event: PointerEventPayload): void {
        if (!event.onCanvas) return;

        this.activePointerCount++;
        const btnSize = 50;

        if (event.y < btnSize) {
            if (event.x < btnSize) {
                this.options.togglePause();
                return;
            }
            if (
                event.x > this.options.getStageWidth() / 2 - btnSize / 2 &&
                event.x < this.options.getStageWidth() / 2 + btnSize / 2
            ) {
                this.options.toggleSound();
                return;
            }
            if (event.x > this.options.getStageWidth() - btnSize) {
                this.options.toggleMenu();
                return;
            }
        }

        if (!this.options.isRunning()) return;

        if (this.updateSpeedFromEvent(event)) {
            this.isUpdatingSpeed = true;
        } else if (event.onCanvas) {
            this.options.launchShell(event);
        }
    }

    public handlePointerEnd(_event: PointerEventPayload): void {
        this.activePointerCount--;
        this.isUpdatingSpeed = false;
    }

    public handlePointerMove(event: PointerEventPayload): void {
        if (!this.options.isRunning()) return;

        if (this.isUpdatingSpeed) {
            this.updateSpeedFromEvent(event);
        }
    }

    public handleKeydown(event: KeyboardEvent): void {
        switch (event.code) {
            case 'Escape':
                this.options.toggleMenu(false);
                break;
            case 'KeyO':
                this.options.toggleMenu();
                break;
            case 'KeyP':
                this.options.togglePause();
                break;
            case 'KeyS':
                this.options.toggleSound();
                break;
            default:
                break;
        }
    }

    private updateSpeedFromEvent(event: PointerEventPayload): boolean {
        if (this.isUpdatingSpeed || event.y >= this.options.getStageHeight() - 44) {
            // On phones it's hard to hit the edge pixels in order to set speed at 0 or 1,
            // so some padding is provided to make that easier.
            const edge: number = 16;
            const newSpeed: number = (event.x - edge) / (this.options.getStageWidth() - edge * 2);
            this.options.setSimulationSpeed(Math.min(Math.max(newSpeed, 0), 1));

            // show speed bar after an update
            this.options.showSpeedBar();

            // If we updated the speed, return true
            return true;
        }
        // Return false if the speed wasn't updated
        return false;
    }
}

// FILE NAME: : ParticlePatterns.ts 
// TYPE     : .TS
// PATH     : src/engine/ParticlePatterns.ts
// PROCESSED: 2026-10-09T19:04:33.965Z
//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE-PATH: src/engine/ParticlePatterns.ts

// src/engine/ParticlePatterns.ts

export type ArcParticleFactory = (angle: number) => void;

export type BurstParticleFactory = (angle: number, speedMultiplier: number) => void;

export class ParticlePatterns {
    public static createArc(
        start: number,
        arcLength: number,
        count: number,
        randomness: number,
        particleFactory: ArcParticleFactory,
    ): void {
        const angleDelta: number = arcLength / count;

        const end: number = start + arcLength - angleDelta * 0.5;

        if (end > start) {
            for (let angle: number = start; angle < end; angle = angle + angleDelta) {
                particleFactory(angle + Math.random() * angleDelta * randomness);
            }

            return;
        }

        for (let angle: number = start; angle > end; angle = angle + angleDelta) {
            particleFactory(angle + Math.random() * angleDelta * randomness);
        }
    }

    public static createBurst(
        count: number,
        particleFactory: BurstParticleFactory,
        startAngle: number = 0,
        arcLength: number = Math.PI * 2,
    ): void {
        const radius: number = 0.5 * Math.sqrt(count / Math.PI);

        const circumference: number = 2 * radius * Math.PI;

        const halfCircumference: number = circumference / 2;

        for (let index: number = 0; index <= halfCircumference; index++) {
            const ringAngle: number = (index / halfCircumference) * Math.PI * 0.5;

            const ringSize: number = Math.cos(ringAngle);

            const partsPerFullRing: number = circumference * ringSize;

            const partsPerArc: number = partsPerFullRing * (arcLength / (Math.PI * 2));

            const angleIncrement: number = (Math.PI * 2) / partsPerFullRing;

            const angleOffset: number = Math.random() * angleIncrement + startAngle;

            const maxRandomAngleOffset: number = angleIncrement * 0.33;

            for (let partIndex: number = 0; partIndex < partsPerArc; partIndex++) {
                const randomAngleOffset: number = Math.random() * maxRandomAngleOffset;

                const angle: number = angleIncrement * partIndex + angleOffset + randomAngleOffset;

                particleFactory(angle, ringSize);
            }
        }
    }
}

// FILE NAME: : ParticleSimulation.ts 
// TYPE     : .TS
// PATH     : src/engine/ParticleSimulation.ts
// PROCESSED: 2026-10-09T19:04:33.965Z
//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE-PATH: src/engine/ParticleSimulation.ts

import { type ParticleColor, INVISIBLE } from '../constants/colors';
import type { SparkManager, SparkParticle } from './particles/Spark';
import type { StarManager, StarParticle } from './particles/Star';

export interface ParticleSimulationOptions {
    readonly stars: StarManager;
    readonly sparks: SparkManager;

    readonly particleColors: readonly ParticleColor[];

    readonly gravity: number;

    isLowQuality(): boolean;
}

export class ParticleSimulation {
    private readonly options: ParticleSimulationOptions;

    private currentFrame: number = 0;

    public constructor(options: ParticleSimulationOptions) {
        this.options = options;
    }

    public update(timeStep: number, speed: number): void {
        this.currentFrame++;

        const starDrag: number = 1 - (1 - this.options.stars.airDrag) * speed;
        const starDragHeavy: number = 1 - (1 - this.options.stars.airDragHeavy) * speed;
        const sparkDrag: number = 1 - (1 - this.options.sparks.airDrag) * speed;
        const gAcc: number = (timeStep / 1000) * this.options.gravity;

        this.options.particleColors.forEach((color: string): void => {
            // Stars
            const stars: StarParticle[] = this.options.stars.active[color];

            for (let i: number = stars.length - 1; i >= 0; i = i - 1) {
                const star: StarParticle = stars[i];

                // Only update each star once per frame. Since color can change, it's possible
                // a star could update twice without this, leading to a "jump".
                if (star.updateFrame === this.currentFrame) continue;

                star.updateFrame = this.currentFrame;

                star.life -= timeStep;

                if (star.life <= 0) {
                    stars.splice(i, 1);

                    this.options.stars.returnInstance(star);
                } else {
                    const burnRate: number = Math.pow(star.life / star.fullLife, 0.5);
                    const burnRateInverse: number = 1 - burnRate;

                    star.prevX = star.x;
                    star.prevY = star.y;

                    star.x += star.speedX * speed;
                    star.y += star.speedY * speed;

                    // Apply air drag if star isn't "heavy". The heavy property is used for the
                    // shell comets.
                    if (!star.heavy) {
                        star.speedX *= starDrag;
                        star.speedY *= starDrag;
                    } else {
                        star.speedX *= starDragHeavy;
                        star.speedY *= starDragHeavy;
                    }
                    star.speedY += gAcc;

                    if (star.spinRadius) {
                        star.spinAngle += star.spinSpeed * speed;
                        star.x += Math.sin(star.spinAngle) * star.spinRadius * speed;
                        star.y += Math.cos(star.spinAngle) * star.spinRadius * speed;
                    }

                    if (star.sparkFreq) {
                        star.sparkTimer -= timeStep;
                        while (star.sparkTimer < 0) {
                            star.sparkTimer += star.sparkFreq * 0.75 + star.sparkFreq * burnRateInverse * 4;
                            this.options.sparks.add(
                                star.x,
                                star.y,
                                star.sparkColor,
                                Math.random() * (Math.PI * 2),
                                Math.random() * star.sparkSpeed * burnRate,
                                star.sparkLife * 0.8 + Math.random() * star.sparkLifeVariation * star.sparkLife,
                            );
                        }
                    }

                    // Handle star transitions
                    if (star.life < star.transitionTime) {
                        if (star.secondColor && !star.colorChanged) {
                            star.colorChanged = true;
                            star.color = star.secondColor;
                            stars.splice(i, 1);

                            this.options.stars.active[star.secondColor].push(star);

                            if (star.secondColor === INVISIBLE) {
                                star.sparkFreq = 0;
                            }
                        }

                        if (star.strobe) {
                            // Strobes in the following pattern: on:off:off:on:off:off in increments
                            // of `strobeFreq` ms.
                            star.visible = Math.floor(star.life / star.strobeFreq) % 3 === 0;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // Sparks
            const sparks: SparkParticle[] = this.options.sparks.active[color];

            for (let i: number = sparks.length - 1; i >= 0; i = i - 1) {
                const spark: SparkParticle = sparks[i];
                spark.life -= timeStep;

                if (spark.life <= 0) {
                    sparks.splice(i, 1);

                    this.options.sparks.returnInstance(spark);
                } else {
                    spark.prevX = spark.x;
                    spark.prevY = spark.y;
                    spark.x += spark.speedX * speed;
                    spark.y += spark.speedY * speed;
                    spark.speedX *= sparkDrag;
                    spark.speedY *= sparkDrag;
                    spark.speedY += gAcc;
                }
            }
        });
    }
}

// FILE NAME: : SequenceController.ts 
// TYPE     : .TS
// PATH     : src/engine/SequenceController.ts
// PROCESSED: 2026-10-09T19:04:33.965Z
//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE-PATH: src/engine/SequenceController.ts

import type { ConfiguredShellName, ShellFactory, ShellName, ShellOptions } from './ShellTypes';

import type { Shell } from './Shell';

export interface RandomShellPosition {
    size: number;
    x: number;
    height: number;
}

export interface SequenceControllerDependencies {
    readonly isDesktop: boolean;
    readonly isHeader: boolean;

    getSelectedShellSize(): number;

    isFinaleEnabled(): boolean;

    getConfiguredShellName(): ConfiguredShellName;

    getShellFactory(shellName: ShellName): ShellFactory;

    createShell(options: ShellOptions): Shell;

    createShellFromConfig(size: number): ShellOptions;

    createRandomShell(size: number): ShellOptions;

    getRandomFastShellFactory(): ShellFactory;

    getRandomShellPosition(): RandomShellPosition;

    getCurrentTime(): number;

    schedule(callback: () => void, delay: number): number;

    random(): number;
}

export class SequenceController {
    private readonly dependencies: SequenceControllerDependencies;
    private isFirstSequence: boolean = true;
    private currentFinaleCount: number = 0;
    private readonly finaleCount: number = 32;
    private readonly smallBarrageCooldownMs: number = 15000;
    private lastSmallBarrageTime: number;

    public constructor(dependencies: SequenceControllerDependencies) {
        this.dependencies = dependencies;
        this.lastSmallBarrageTime = dependencies.getCurrentTime();
    }

    public startNextSequence(): number {
        if (this.isFirstSequence) {
            this.isFirstSequence = false;

            if (this.dependencies.isHeader) {
                return this.sequenceTwoRandom();
            }

            const crysanthemumFactory: ShellFactory = this.dependencies.getShellFactory('Crysanthemum');

            const shell: Shell = this.dependencies.createShell(
                crysanthemumFactory(this.dependencies.getSelectedShellSize()),
            );

            shell.launch(0.5, 0.5);

            return 2400;
        }

        if (this.dependencies.isFinaleEnabled()) {
            this.sequenceRandomFastShell();

            if (this.currentFinaleCount < this.finaleCount) {
                this.currentFinaleCount++;

                return 170;
            }

            this.currentFinaleCount = 0;

            return 6000;
        }

        const randomValue: number = this.dependencies.random();

        const smallBarrageReady: boolean =
            this.dependencies.getCurrentTime() - this.lastSmallBarrageTime > this.smallBarrageCooldownMs;

        if (randomValue < 0.08 && smallBarrageReady) {
            return this.sequenceSmallBarrage();
        }

        if (randomValue < 0.1) {
            return this.sequencePyramid();
        }

        if (randomValue < 0.6 && !this.dependencies.isHeader) {
            return this.sequenceRandomShell();
        }

        if (randomValue < 0.8) {
            return this.sequenceTwoRandom();
        }

        return this.sequenceTriple();
    }

    private sequenceRandomShell(): number {
        const position: RandomShellPosition = this.dependencies.getRandomShellPosition();

        const shell: Shell = this.dependencies.createShell(this.dependencies.createShellFromConfig(position.size));

        shell.launch(position.x, position.height);

        let extraDelay: number = shell.starLife;

        if (shell.fallingLeaves) {
            extraDelay = 4600;
        }

        return 900 + this.dependencies.random() * 600 + extraDelay;
    }

    private sequenceRandomFastShell(): number {
        const shellFactory: ShellFactory = this.dependencies.getRandomFastShellFactory();

        const position: RandomShellPosition = this.dependencies.getRandomShellPosition();

        const shell: Shell = this.dependencies.createShell(shellFactory(position.size));

        shell.launch(position.x, position.height);

        const extraDelay: number = shell.starLife;

        return 900 + this.dependencies.random() * 600 + extraDelay;
    }

    private sequenceTwoRandom(): number {
        const positionOne: RandomShellPosition = this.dependencies.getRandomShellPosition();

        const positionTwo: RandomShellPosition = this.dependencies.getRandomShellPosition();

        const shellOne: Shell = this.dependencies.createShell(
            this.dependencies.createShellFromConfig(positionOne.size),
        );

        const shellTwo: Shell = this.dependencies.createShell(
            this.dependencies.createShellFromConfig(positionTwo.size),
        );

        const leftOffset: number = this.dependencies.random() * 0.2 - 0.1;

        const rightOffset: number = this.dependencies.random() * 0.2 - 0.1;

        shellOne.launch(0.3 + leftOffset, positionOne.height);

        this.dependencies.schedule((): void => {
            shellTwo.launch(0.7 + rightOffset, positionTwo.height);
        }, 100);

        let extraDelay: number = Math.max(shellOne.starLife, shellTwo.starLife);

        if (shellOne.fallingLeaves || shellTwo.fallingLeaves) {
            extraDelay = 4600;
        }

        return 900 + this.dependencies.random() * 600 + extraDelay;
    }

    private sequenceTriple(): number {
        const shellFactory: ShellFactory = this.dependencies.getRandomFastShellFactory();

        const baseSize: number = this.dependencies.getSelectedShellSize();

        const smallSize: number = Math.max(0, baseSize - 1.25);

        const centerOffset: number = this.dependencies.random() * 0.08 - 0.04;

        const centerShell: Shell = this.dependencies.createShell(shellFactory(baseSize));

        centerShell.launch(0.5 + centerOffset, 0.7);

        const leftDelay: number = 1000 + this.dependencies.random() * 400;

        const rightDelay: number = 1000 + this.dependencies.random() * 400;

        this.dependencies.schedule((): void => {
            const offset: number = this.dependencies.random() * 0.08 - 0.04;

            const leftShell: Shell = this.dependencies.createShell(shellFactory(smallSize));

            leftShell.launch(0.2 + offset, 0.1);
        }, leftDelay);

        this.dependencies.schedule((): void => {
            const offset: number = this.dependencies.random() * 0.08 - 0.04;

            const rightShell: Shell = this.dependencies.createShell(shellFactory(smallSize));

            rightShell.launch(0.8 + offset, 0.1);
        }, rightDelay);

        return 4000;
    }

    private sequencePyramid(): number {
        const barrageCountHalf: number = this.dependencies.isDesktop ? 7 : 4;

        const largeSize: number = this.dependencies.getSelectedShellSize();

        const smallSize: number = Math.max(0, largeSize - 3);

        const randomMainShell: ShellFactory =
            this.dependencies.random() < 0.78
                ? this.dependencies.getShellFactory('Crysanthemum')
                : this.dependencies.getShellFactory('Ring');

        const randomSpecialShell: ShellFactory = (size: number): ShellOptions =>
            this.dependencies.createRandomShell(size);

        const configuredShellName: ConfiguredShellName = this.dependencies.getConfiguredShellName();

        const createPyramidShell: (useSpecial: boolean) => Shell = (useSpecial: boolean): Shell => {
            const shellSize: number = useSpecial ? largeSize : smallSize;

            const shellFactory: ShellFactory =
                configuredShellName === 'Random'
                    ? useSpecial
                        ? randomSpecialShell
                        : randomMainShell
                    : this.dependencies.getShellFactory(configuredShellName);

            return this.dependencies.createShell(shellFactory(shellSize));
        };

        const launchShell: (x: number, useSpecial: boolean) => void = (x: number, useSpecial: boolean): void => {
            const shell: Shell = createPyramidShell(useSpecial);

            const height: number = x <= 0.5 ? x / 0.5 : (1 - x) / 0.5;

            shell.launch(x, useSpecial ? 0.75 : height * 0.42);
        };

        let count: number = 0;
        let delay: number = 0;

        while (count <= barrageCountHalf) {
            if (count === barrageCountHalf) {
                this.dependencies.schedule((): void => {
                    launchShell(0.5, true);
                }, delay);
            } else {
                const offset: number = (count / barrageCountHalf) * 0.5;

                const delayOffset: number = this.dependencies.random() * 30 + 30;

                this.dependencies.schedule((): void => {
                    launchShell(offset, false);
                }, delay);

                this.dependencies.schedule((): void => {
                    launchShell(1 - offset, false);
                }, delay + delayOffset);
            }

            count++;
            delay += 200;
        }

        return 3400 + barrageCountHalf * 250;
    }

    private sequenceSmallBarrage(): number {
        this.lastSmallBarrageTime = this.dependencies.getCurrentTime();

        const barrageCount: number = this.dependencies.isDesktop ? 11 : 5;

        const specialIndex: number = this.dependencies.isDesktop ? 3 : 1;

        const shellSize: number = Math.max(0, this.dependencies.getSelectedShellSize() - 2);

        const randomMainShell: ShellFactory =
            this.dependencies.random() < 0.78
                ? this.dependencies.getShellFactory('Crysanthemum')
                : this.dependencies.getShellFactory('Ring');

        const randomSpecialShell: ShellFactory = this.dependencies.getRandomFastShellFactory();

        const configuredShellName: ConfiguredShellName = this.dependencies.getConfiguredShellName();

        const createBarrageShell: (useSpecial: boolean) => Shell = (useSpecial: boolean): Shell => {
            const shellFactory: ShellFactory =
                configuredShellName === 'Random'
                    ? useSpecial
                        ? randomSpecialShell
                        : randomMainShell
                    : this.dependencies.getShellFactory(configuredShellName);

            return this.dependencies.createShell(shellFactory(shellSize));
        };

        const launchShell: (x: number, useSpecial: boolean) => void = (x: number, useSpecial: boolean): void => {
            const shell: Shell = createBarrageShell(useSpecial);

            const height: number = (Math.cos(x * 5 * Math.PI + Math.PI * 0.5) + 1) / 2;

            shell.launch(x, height * 0.75);
        };

        let count: number = 0;
        let delay: number = 0;

        while (count < barrageCount) {
            if (count === 0) {
                launchShell(0.5, false);
                count += 1;
            } else {
                const offset: number = (count + 1) / barrageCount / 2;

                const delayOffset: number = this.dependencies.random() * 30 + 30;

                const useSpecial: boolean = count === specialIndex;

                this.dependencies.schedule((): void => {
                    launchShell(0.5 + offset, useSpecial);
                }, delay);

                this.dependencies.schedule((): void => {
                    launchShell(0.5 - offset, useSpecial);
                }, delay + delayOffset);

                count += 2;
            }

            delay += 200;
        }

        return 3400 + barrageCount * 120;
    }
}
