// PROCESSED: 2026-10-09T19:04:33.965Z
//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE-PATH: src/engine/particles/Star.ts

// src/engine/particles/Star.ts

import { createParticleCollection, type ParticleCollection } from './ParticleCollection.js';
import { COLOR, type ParticleColor, type VisibleColor, isVisibleColor } from '../../constants/colors';

export type StarDeathHandler = (star: StarParticle) => void;

export class StarParticle {
    public visible: boolean = true;
    public heavy: boolean = false;

    public x: number = 0;
    public y: number = 0;
    public prevX: number = 0;
    public prevY: number = 0;

    public color: ParticleColor = COLOR.White;

    public speedX: number = 0;
    public speedY: number = 0;

    public life: number = 0;
    public fullLife: number = 0;

    public updateFrame: number = -1;

    public spinAngle: number = 0;
    public spinSpeed: number = 0.8;
    public spinRadius: number = 0;

    public sparkFreq: number = 0;
    public sparkSpeed: number = 1;
    public sparkTimer: number = 0;
    public sparkColor: VisibleColor = COLOR.White;
    public sparkLife: number = 750;
    public sparkLifeVariation: number = 0.25;

    public strobe: boolean = false;
    public strobeFreq: number = 0;

    public secondColor: ParticleColor | null = null;
    public transitionTime: number = 0;
    public colorChanged: boolean = false;

    public onDeath: StarDeathHandler | null = null;

    public reset(
        x: number,
        y: number,
        color: ParticleColor,
        angle: number,
        speed: number,
        life: number,
        speedOffsetX = 0,
        speedOffsetY = 0,
    ): void {
        this.visible = true;
        this.heavy = false;

        this.x = x;
        this.y = y;
        this.prevX = x;
        this.prevY = y;

        this.color = color;

        this.speedX = Math.sin(angle) * speed + speedOffsetX;
        this.speedY = Math.cos(angle) * speed + speedOffsetY;

        this.life = life;
        this.fullLife = life;

        this.updateFrame = -1;

        this.spinAngle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
        this.spinSpeed = 0.8;
        this.spinRadius = 0;

        this.sparkFreq = 0;
        this.sparkSpeed = 1;
        this.sparkTimer = 0;

        /*
         * Spark colors must be visible because SparkManager.add()
         * should accept VisibleColor only.
         */
        this.sparkColor = isVisibleColor(color) ? color : COLOR.White;

        this.sparkLife = 750;
        this.sparkLifeVariation = 0.25;

        this.strobe = false;
        this.strobeFreq = 0;

        this.secondColor = null;
        this.transitionTime = 0;
        this.colorChanged = false;

        this.onDeath = null;
    }
}

export class StarManager {
    public drawWidth: number = 3;
    public airDrag: number = 0.98;
    public airDragHeavy: number = 0.992;

    public readonly active: ParticleCollection<StarParticle>;

    private readonly pool: StarParticle[] = [];

    public constructor(colors: readonly ParticleColor[]) {
        this.active = createParticleCollection<StarParticle>(colors) as ParticleCollection<StarParticle>;
    }

    public add(
        x: number,
        y: number,
        color: ParticleColor,
        angle: number,
        speed: number,
        life: number,
        speedOffsetX = 0,
        speedOffsetY = 0,
    ): StarParticle {
        const instance: StarParticle = this.pool.pop() ?? new StarParticle();

        instance.reset(x, y, color, angle, speed, life, speedOffsetX, speedOffsetY);

        this.active[color].push(instance);

        return instance;
    }

    public returnInstance(instance: StarParticle): void {
        instance.onDeath?.(instance);

        instance.onDeath = null;
        instance.secondColor = null;
        instance.transitionTime = 0;
        instance.colorChanged = false;

        this.pool.push(instance);
    }
}

export class AppNodes {

    public readonly closeMenuBtn: HTMLElement;

    public readonly pauseBtn: HTMLElement;

    public readonly soundBtn: HTMLElement;
    public readonly soundBtnSVG: SVGUseElement;

    public readonly shellType: HTMLSelectElement;
    public readonly shellTypeLabel: HTMLLabelElement;

    public readonly shellSize: HTMLSelectElement;
    public readonly shellSizeLabel: HTMLLabelElement;

    public readonly quality: HTMLSelectElement;
    public readonly qualityLabel: HTMLLabelElement;

    public readonly skyLighting: HTMLSelectElement;
    public readonly skyLightingLabel: HTMLLabelElement;

    public readonly scaleFactor: HTMLSelectElement;
    public readonly scaleFactorLabel: HTMLLabelElement;

    public readonly autoLaunch: HTMLInputElement;
    public readonly autoLaunchLabel: HTMLLabelElement;

    public readonly finaleModeFormOption: HTMLElement;
    public readonly finaleMode: HTMLInputElement;
    public readonly finaleModeLabel: HTMLLabelElement;

    public readonly hideControls: HTMLInputElement;
    public readonly hideControlsLabel: HTMLLabelElement;

    public readonly fullscreenLabel: HTMLLabelElement;

    public readonly longExposure: HTMLInputElement;
    public readonly longExposureLabel: HTMLLabelElement;

    public readonly helpModal: HTMLElement;
    public readonly helpModalOverlay: HTMLElement;
    public readonly helpModalHeader: HTMLElement;
    public readonly helpModalBody: HTMLElement;
    public readonly helpModalCloseBtn: HTMLButtonElement;

    public constructor(documentRef: Document = document) {
        this.stageContainer = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.stageContainer);

        this.canvasContainer = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.canvasContainer);

        this.trailsCanvas = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLCanvasElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.trailsCanvas);

        this.mainCanvas = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLCanvasElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.mainCanvas);

        this.controls = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.controls);

        this.menu = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.menu);

        this.menuInnerWrap = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.menuInnerWrap);

        this.settingsBtn = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.settingsBtn);

        this.closeMenuBtn = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.closeMenuBtn);

        this.pauseBtn = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.pauseBtn);

        this.pauseBtnSVG = this.getRequiredElement<SVGUseElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.pauseBtnSVG);

        this.soundBtn = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.soundBtn);

        this.soundBtnSVG = this.getRequiredElement<SVGUseElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.soundBtnSVG);

        this.shellType = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLSelectElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.shellType);

        this.shellTypeLabel = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLLabelElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.shellTypeLabel);

        this.shellSize = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLSelectElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.shellSize);

        this.shellSizeLabel = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLLabelElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.shellSizeLabel);

        this.quality = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLSelectElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.quality);

        this.qualityLabel = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLLabelElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.qualityLabel);

        this.skyLighting = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLSelectElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.skyLighting);

        this.skyLightingLabel = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLLabelElement>(
            documentRef,
            APP_NODE_SELECTORS.skyLightingLabel,
        );

        this.scaleFactor = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLSelectElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.scaleFactor);

        this.scaleFactorLabel = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLLabelElement>(
            documentRef,
            APP_NODE_SELECTORS.scaleFactorLabel,
        );

        this.autoLaunch = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLInputElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.autoLaunch);

        this.autoLaunchLabel = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLLabelElement>(
            documentRef,
            APP_NODE_SELECTORS.autoLaunchLabel,
        );

        this.finaleModeFormOption = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLElement>(
            documentRef,
            APP_NODE_SELECTORS.finaleModeFormOption,
        );

        this.finaleMode = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLInputElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.finaleMode);

        this.finaleModeLabel = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLLabelElement>(
            documentRef,
            APP_NODE_SELECTORS.finaleModeLabel,
        );

        this.hideControls = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLInputElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.hideControls);

        this.hideControlsLabel = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLLabelElement>(
            documentRef,
            APP_NODE_SELECTORS.hideControlsLabel,
        );

        this.fullscreenFormOption = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLElement>(
            documentRef,
            APP_NODE_SELECTORS.fullscreenFormOption,
        );

        this.fullscreen = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLInputElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.fullscreen);

        this.fullscreenLabel = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLLabelElement>(
            documentRef,
            APP_NODE_SELECTORS.fullscreenLabel,
        );

        this.longExposure = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLInputElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.longExposure);

        this.longExposureLabel = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLLabelElement>(
            documentRef,
            APP_NODE_SELECTORS.longExposureLabel,
        );

        this.helpModal = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.helpModal);

        this.helpModalOverlay = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.helpModalOverlay);

        this.helpModalHeader = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.helpModalHeader);

        this.helpModalBody = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLElement>(documentRef, APP_NODE_SELECTORS.helpModalBody);

        this.helpModalCloseBtn = this.getRequiredElement<HTMLButtonElement>(
            documentRef,
            APP_NODE_SELECTORS.helpModalCloseBtn,
        );
    }

    public getHelpNode(key: HelpNodeKey): HTMLLabelElement {
        return this[key];
    }

    private getRequiredElement<TElement extends Element>(documentRef: Document, selector: string): TElement {
        const element: TElement | null = documentRef.querySelector<TElement>(selector);

        if (element === null) {
            throw new Error(`Required DOM element was not found: ${selector}`);
        }

        return element;
    }
}

// oxlint-disable typescript/no-explicit-any

import { Pane, type FolderApi } from 'tweakpane';
import * as EssentialsPlugin from '@tweakpane/plugin-essentials';

const PANE_CONTAINER_SELECTOR: string = '.pane';

export class PerformancePane {
    private readonly pane: Pane;
    private readonly performanceFolder: FolderApi;
    private readonly fpsGraph: any;

    public constructor() {
        const container: HTMLElement | null = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(PANE_CONTAINER_SELECTOR);

        if (container === null) {
            throw new Error(`Performance pane container was not found: ${PANE_CONTAINER_SELECTOR}`);
        }

        // Configure Tweakpane Dashboard and Elements
        this.pane = new Pane({
            title: 'Fireworks Debug Console',
            expanded: true,
            container,
        });

        this.pane.registerPlugin(EssentialsPlugin);

        this.performanceFolder = this.pane.addFolder({
            title: 'Performance',
            expanded: true,
        });

        this.fpsGraph = this.performanceFolder.addBlade({
            view: 'fpsgraph',
            label: 'Performance FPS',
            lineCount: 2,
        }) as any;

        /*
         * Ensure the pane remains visible above the canvas and application UI.
         * Adjust this value only if your application has a higher required
         * overlay layer.
         */
        this.pane.element.style.zIndex = '1000';
    }

    public begin(): void {
        this.fpsGraph.begin();
    }

    public end(): void {
        this.fpsGraph.end();
    }

    public destroy(): void {
        this.pane.dispose();
    }
}

export const COLOR_BLACK = '#000';

// Validate format using a regex pattern
const HEX_REGEX = /^([0-9a-fA-F]{3}|[0-9a-fA-F]{6}|[0-9a-fA-F]{8})\$/;

interface ColorPalette {
    readonly Red: '#FF2400'; // Strontium / Lithium salts
    readonly Orange: '#FFA500'; // Calcium salts

    readonly Blue: '#1E7FFF'; // Copper compounds (hardest to produce)

    readonly White: '#FFFFFF'; // Magnesium / Aluminum burn

    readonly Aqua: '#00FFFF'; // Copper + Barium mix
    readonly Violet: '#9400D3'; // High-temperature Potassium / Copper variant
    readonly Bronze: '#B87333'; // Modified Calcium / Iron composition
    readonly Charcoal: '#8B5A2B'; // Slow-burning Carbon particles (Willow trail)
}

    readonly Trail: 'rgba(255, 140, 20, 0.11)'; // FadeAmber

// Automatically extract the type safely from the object structure
export type ColorName = keyof ColorPalette;

export type RandomColor = '_RANDOM_';
export const RANDOM: RandomColor = '_RANDOM_';

// Special invisible color (not rendered, and therefore not in COLOR map)
export type InvisibleColor = '_INVISIBLE_';
export const INVISIBLE: InvisibleColor = '_INVISIBLE_';

export type VisibleColor = ColorPalette[ColorName];

export type ParticleColor = VisibleColor | InvisibleColor;

export type ColorPair = [VisibleColor, VisibleColor];

export type ShellColor = RandomColor | ParticleColor | ColorPair;

// Helper type to generate a union of numbers from 0 to N-1
export type Enumerate<N extends number, Acc extends number[] = []> = Acc['length'] extends N
    ? Acc[number]
    : Enumerate<N, [...Acc, Acc['length']]>;

// Type that limits numbers between a Min and Max (inclusive)
export type IntRange<F extends number, T extends number> = Exclude<Enumerate<T>, Enumerate<F>>;

// 0 to 255 range (using 256 since the upper bound is exclusive in Enumerate)
export type Byte = IntRange<0, 256>;

export type RGBComponents = 'r' | 'g' | 'b';

// Object structure type requiring absolute compile-time Bytes
export type IRGBColor = { [K in RGBComponents]: Byte };

/**
 * RGBColor class
 *
 * @param { Byte }
 */
export class RGBColor implements Record<RGBComponents, number> {
    // Internally tracked strictly as Byte for data integrity
    private _r!: Byte;
    private _g!: Byte;
    private _b!: Byte;

    // Public property to control case of hex string
    public uppercase: boolean = true;

    constructor(r?: number, g?: number, b?: number);
    constructor(hexString?: string);
    constructor(rOrHex?: number | string, g?: number, b?: number) {
        if (typeof rOrHex === 'string') {
            // Direct assignment to the hex setter
            this.hex = rOrHex;
        } else {
            // Fallback to numeric logic using the nullish coalescing operator
            this._r = this.validate(rOrHex ?? 0);
            this._g = this.validate(g ?? 0);
            this._b = this.validate(b ?? 0);
        }
    }

    // Getters and setters accept generic numbers to allow open assignment
    get r(): number {
        return this._r;
    }
    set r(value: number) {
        this._r = this.validate(value);
    }

    get g(): number {
        return this._g;
    }
    set g(value: number) {
        this._g = this.validate(value);
    }

    get b(): number {
        return this._b;
    }
    set b(value: number) {
        this._b = this.validate(value);
    }

    // Validates the value at runtime and safely casts it to Byte for internal storage
    private validate(value: number): Byte {
        // Force decimals to integers (e.g., 12.5 -> 13)
        const integerValue: number = Math.round(value);

        // Clamp the integer securely between 0 and 255
        const clampedValue: number = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, integerValue));

        // Return safely cast as your strict internal Byte type
        return clampedValue as Byte;
    }

    /**
     * Converts the RGB color to a hexadecimal string.
     */
    public get hex(): string {
        const hex: string = [this._r, this._g, this._b]
            .map((component: Byte): string => component.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
            .join('');

        return `#${this.uppercase ? hex.toUpperCase() : hex}`;
    }

    /**
     * Parses a hex string and updates the r, g, and b components.
     * Supports both 3-digit (#F80) and 6-digit (#FF8000) formats, with or without '#'.
     *
     * @param { string } hex - The hex string to convert to RGB
     * @todo Add support for 8-digit formats
     */
    public set hex(hex: string) {
        // Clean the string by removing the leading '#' if present
        const cleanHex: string = hex.trim().replace(/^#/, '');

        // Validate format using a regex pattern
        if (!HEX_REGEX.test(cleanHex)) {
            throw new Error(`Invalid hex color string: "${hex}". Must be a 3 or 6 digit hex format.`);
        }

        let rStr: string;
        let gStr: string;
        let bStr: string;

        // Extract components based on length
        switch (cleanHex.length) {
            case 3:
                // Shorthand format: expand "F80" into "FF", "88", "00"
                rStr = cleanHex[0] + cleanHex[0];
                gStr = cleanHex[1] + cleanHex[1];
                bStr = cleanHex[2] + cleanHex[2];
                break;
            // @ts-expect-error - intentional fallthrough
            case 8:
                // Future RGBA Expansion
                console.warn(`RGBA not implemented yet, truncating hex string to ${cleanHex.substring(0, 6)}`);
            default:
                // case 6:
                // Full format: "FF8000" -> "FF", "80", "00"
                rStr = cleanHex.substring(0, 2);
                gStr = cleanHex.substring(2, 4);
                bStr = cleanHex.substring(4, 6);
        }

        // Parse strings into integers (Base 16) and route through setters for validation
        this.r = parseInt(rStr, 16);
        this.g = parseInt(gStr, 16);
        this.b = parseInt(bStr, 16);
    }
}

export type ColorTuple = Record<ColorCode, RGBColor>;

// Constant derivations
const COLOR_NAMES: readonly ColorName[] = [
    'Red',
    'Orange',
    'Gold',
    'Yellow',
    'Green',
    'Jade',
    'Blue',
    'Indigo',
    'Purple',
    'Silver',
    'White',
    'Pink',
    'Aqua',
    'Violet',
    'Bronze',
    'Charcoal',
];

const VISIBLE_COLORS: readonly VisibleColor[] = [
    COLOR.Red, // Strontium / Lithium salts
    COLOR.Orange, // Calcium salts
    COLOR.Gold, // Charcoal / Iron / Lampblack
    COLOR.Yellow, // Sodium salts
    COLOR.Green, // Barium salts
    COLOR.Jade, // Barium / Copper mix variant
    COLOR.Blue, // Copper compounds (hardest to produce)
    COLOR.Indigo, // Specialized Copper combinations
    COLOR.Purple, // Strontium + Copper mix
    COLOR.Silver, // Aluminum / Titanium / Magnesium flakes
    COLOR.White, // Magnesium / Aluminum burn

    // Advanced Pyrotechnic Colors
    COLOR.Pink, // Strontium + Potassium perchlorate mix
    COLOR.Aqua, // Copper + Barium mix
    COLOR.Violet, // High-temperature Potassium / Copper variant
    COLOR.Bronze, // Modified Calcium / Iron composition
    COLOR.Charcoal, // Slow-burning Carbon particles (Willow trail)
];

const PARTICLE_COLORS: readonly ParticleColor[] = [...VISIBLE_COLORS, INVISIBLE];

/**
 * Transitional aliases.
 * Remove after every consumer uses VISIBLE_COLORS and PARTICLE_COLORS.
 */
const COLOR_CODES: readonly VisibleColor[] = VISIBLE_COLORS;

// Invisible stars need an identifier, even through they won't be rendered - physics still apply.
const COLOR_CODES_W_INVIS: readonly ParticleColor[] = PARTICLE_COLORS;

export type ColorCode = ParticleColor;

// Map of color codes to their index in the array. Useful for quickly determining
// if a color has already been updated in a loop.
const COLOR_CODE_INDEXES: Record<string, number> = COLOR_CODES_W_INVIS.reduce<Record<string, number>>(
    (obj: Record<string, number>, code: string, i: number): Record<string, number> => {
        obj[code] = i;
        return obj;
    },
    {},
);

// Tuples is a map keys by color codes (hex) with values of { r, g, b } tuples (still just objects).
const COLOR_TUPLES: ColorTuple = COLOR_CODES.reduce<ColorTuple>((obj: ColorTuple, hex: string): ColorTuple => {
    obj[hex] = {
        r: parseInt(hex.substring(1, 3), 16) as Byte,
        g: parseInt(hex.substring(3, 5), 16) as Byte,
        b: parseInt(hex.substring(5, 7), 16) as Byte,
    };
    return obj;
}, {} as ColorTuple);

// Typeguard
function isVisibleColor(color: ParticleColor): color is VisibleColor {
    return color !== INVISIBLE;
}

function isRandomColor(color: ShellColor): color is VisibleColor {
    return color !== RANDOM;
}

    COLOR,
    BURSTCOLOR,
    COLOR_NAMES,
    COLOR_CODES,
    COLOR_CODES_W_INVIS,
    COLOR_CODE_INDEXES,
    COLOR_TUPLES,
    VISIBLE_COLORS,
    PARTICLE_COLORS,
    isVisibleColor,
    isRandomColor,
};

// components/Component.ts

export abstract class Component {
    protected readonly element: HTMLElement;

    protected constructor(element: HTMLElement) {
        this.element = element;
    }

    abstract mount(): void;

    abstract render(): void;

    destroy(): void {
        // Override when a component owns event listeners or other resources.
    }
}

//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-<  END  >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■

// PROCESSED: 2026-10-09T19:04:33.965Z
//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-<  END  >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■

//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE NAME: : Controls.ts 
// TYPE     : .TS
// PATH     : src/components/Controls.ts
// PROCESSED: 2026-10-09T19:04:33.965Z
//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE-PATH: src/components/Controls.ts

// src/components/Controls.ts

import { Component } from './Component';

import type { AppNodes } from '../dom/AppNodes';

import type { AppState } from '../app/AppState';

import type { AppStore } from '../app/AppStore';

export interface ControlsActions {
    onTogglePause(): void;
    onToggleSound(): void;
    onToggleMenu(): void;
}

export class Controls extends Component {
    private readonly nodes: AppNodes;

    private readonly store: AppStore;

    private readonly actions: ControlsActions;

    private unsubscribe: (() => void) | undefined;

    private readonly blockedPointerEventTypes: readonly string[] = [
        'pointerdown',
        'pointerup',
        'pointermove',
        'mousedown',
        'mouseup',
        'mousemove',
        'touchstart',
        'touchend',
        'touchmove',
    ];

    private readonly handlePauseClick = (_event: MouseEvent): void => {
        this.actions.onTogglePause();
    };

    private readonly handleSoundClick = (_event: MouseEvent): void => {
        this.actions.onToggleSound();
    };

    private readonly handleSettingsClick = (_event: MouseEvent): void => {
        this.actions.onToggleMenu();
    };

    private readonly handlePointerEvent = (event: Event): void => {
        event.stopPropagation();
    };

    public constructor(nodes: AppNodes, store: AppStore, actions: ControlsActions) {
        super(nodes.controls);

        this.nodes = nodes;
        this.store = store;
        this.actions = actions;
    }

    public mount(): void {
        this.nodes.pauseBtn.addEventListener('click', this.handlePauseClick);

        this.nodes.soundBtn.addEventListener('click', this.handleSoundClick);

        this.nodes.settingsBtn.addEventListener('click', this.handleSettingsClick);

        for (const eventType of this.blockedPointerEventTypes) {
            this.element.addEventListener(eventType, this.handlePointerEvent, true);
        }

        this.unsubscribe = this.store.subscribe((): void => {
            this.render();
        });

        this.render();
    }

    public render(): void {
        const state: Readonly<AppState> = this.store.state;

        const pauseIcon: string = state.paused ? '#icon-play' : '#icon-pause';

        const soundIcon: string = state.soundEnabled ? '#icon-sound-on' : '#icon-sound-off';

        this.nodes.pauseBtnSVG.setAttribute('href', pauseIcon);

        this.nodes.pauseBtnSVG.setAttribute('xlink:href', pauseIcon);

        this.nodes.soundBtnSVG.setAttribute('href', soundIcon);

        this.nodes.soundBtnSVG.setAttribute('xlink:href', soundIcon);

        this.element.classList.toggle('hide', state.menuOpen || state.config.hideControls);
    }

    public override destroy(): void {
        this.nodes.pauseBtn.removeEventListener('click', this.handlePauseClick);

        this.nodes.soundBtn.removeEventListener('click', this.handleSoundClick);

        this.nodes.settingsBtn.removeEventListener('click', this.handleSettingsClick);

        for (const eventType of this.blockedPointerEventTypes) {
            this.element.removeEventListener(eventType, this.handlePointerEvent, true);
        }

        this.unsubscribe?.();
        this.unsubscribe = undefined;
    }
}

//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-<  END  >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■

// FILE NAME: : HelpModal.ts 
// TYPE     : .TS
// PATH     : src/components/HelpModal.ts
// PROCESSED: 2026-10-09T19:04:33.965Z
//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE-PATH: src/components/HelpModal.ts

import { Component } from './Component';
import { HELP_CONTENT } from '../constants/help';
import type { AppState } from '../app/AppState';
import type { AppStore } from '../app/AppStore';
import type { AppNodes } from '../dom/AppNodes';

export interface HelpModalActions {
    onClose(): void;
}

export class HelpModal extends Component {
    private readonly nodes: AppNodes;

    private readonly store: AppStore;

    private readonly actions: HelpModalActions;

    private unsubscribe: (() => void) | undefined;

    private readonly blockedPointerEventTypes: readonly string[] = [
        'pointerdown',
        'pointerup',
        'pointermove',
        'mousedown',
        'mouseup',
        'mousemove',
        'touchstart',
        'touchend',
        'touchmove',
    ];

    private readonly handleCloseClick: (event: MouseEvent) => void = (_event: MouseEvent): void => {
        this.actions.onClose();
    };

    private readonly handleOverlayClick: (event: MouseEvent) => void = (_event: MouseEvent): void => {
        this.actions.onClose();
    };

    private readonly handlePointerEvent: (event: Event) => void = (event: Event): void => {
        event.stopPropagation();
    };

    public constructor(nodes: AppNodes, store: AppStore, actions: HelpModalActions) {
        super(nodes.helpModal);

        this.nodes = nodes;
        this.store = store;
        this.actions = actions;
    }

    public mount(): void {
        this.nodes.helpModalCloseBtn.addEventListener('click', this.handleCloseClick);

        this.nodes.helpModalOverlay.addEventListener('click', this.handleOverlayClick);

        for (const eventType of this.blockedPointerEventTypes) {
            this.element.addEventListener(eventType, this.handlePointerEvent, true);
        }

        this.unsubscribe = this.store.subscribe((): void => {
            this.render();
        });

        this.render();
    }

    public render(): void {
        const state: Readonly<AppState> = this.store.state;

        const topicKey = state.openHelpTopic;

        this.element.classList.toggle('active', topicKey !== null);

        if (topicKey === null) {
            return;
        }

        const topic = HELP_CONTENT[topicKey];

        this.nodes.helpModalHeader.textContent = topic.header;

        this.nodes.helpModalBody.textContent = topic.body;
    }

    public override destroy(): void {
        this.nodes.helpModalCloseBtn.removeEventListener('click', this.handleCloseClick);

        this.nodes.helpModalOverlay.removeEventListener('click', this.handleOverlayClick);

        for (const eventType of this.blockedPointerEventTypes) {
            this.element.removeEventListener(eventType, this.handlePointerEvent, true);
        }

        this.unsubscribe?.();
        this.unsubscribe = undefined;
    }
}

//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-<  END  >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■

// FILE NAME: : SettingsMenu.ts 
// TYPE     : .TS
// PATH     : src/components/SettingsMenu.ts
// PROCESSED: 2026-10-09T19:04:33.965Z
//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE-PATH: src/components/SettingsMenu.ts

import { Component } from './Component';
import type { AppConfig, AppState, HelpTopicKey } from '../app/AppState';
import type { AppStore } from '../app/AppStore';
import type { AppNodes, HelpNodeKey } from '../dom/AppNodes';
import { isQualityChoice, isSkylightChoice, type QualityChoice, type SkylightChoice } from '../constants/config';

export type SettingsChangeConsoleOutput =
    | {
          readonly kind: 'select';
          readonly className: string;
          readonly value: string;
      }
    | {
          readonly kind: 'input';
          readonly className: string;
          readonly checked: boolean;
      };
export interface SelectOption {
    readonly label: string;
    readonly value: string | number;
}

export interface SettingsMenuOptions {
    readonly shellNameOptions: readonly SelectOption[];
    readonly shellSizeOptions: readonly SelectOption[];
    readonly qualityOptions: readonly SelectOption[];
    readonly skyLightingOptions: readonly SelectOption[];
    readonly scaleFactorOptions: readonly SelectOption[];
    readonly helpNodeToTopic: Readonly<Record<HelpNodeKey, HelpTopicKey>>;
    readonly fullscreenSupported: boolean;
}

export interface SettingsMenuActions {
    onClose(): void;
    onConfigChange(nextConfig: Partial<AppConfig>): void;
    onScaleFactorChange(): void;
    onToggleFullscreen(): void;
    onOpenHelp(topic: HelpTopicKey): void;
}

export class SettingsMenu extends Component {
    private readonly nodes: AppNodes;
    private readonly store: AppStore;
    private readonly options: SettingsMenuOptions;
    private readonly actions: SettingsMenuActions;
    private unsubscribe: (() => void) | undefined;

    private readonly blockedPointerEventTypes: readonly string[] = [
        'pointerdown',
        'pointerup',
        'pointermove',
        'mousedown',
        'mouseup',
        'mousemove',
        'touchstart',
        'touchend',
        'touchmove',
    ];

    private readonly helpHandlers: Map<HTMLLabelElement, (event: MouseEvent) => void> = new Map();

    private readonly handleCloseClick: (event: Event) => void = (_event: MouseEvent): void => {
        this.actions.onClose();
    };

    private readonly handleShellTypeChange: (event: Event) => void = (event: Event): void => {
        this.actions.onConfigChange({
            shell: this.nodes.shellType.value,
        });
        this.logConfigChange(event);
    };

    private readonly handleShellSizeChange: (event: Event) => void = (event: Event): void => {
        this.actions.onConfigChange({
            size: this.nodes.shellSize.value,
        });
        this.logConfigChange(event);
    };

    private readonly handleQualityChange: (event: Event) => void = (event: Event): void => {
        const qualityValue: string = this.nodes.quality.value;

        if (!isQualityChoice(qualityValue)) {
            throw new Error(`Invalid quality value: ${qualityValue}`);
        }

        const quality: QualityChoice = qualityValue;

        this.actions.onConfigChange({
            quality,
        });

        this.logConfigChange(event);
    };

    private readonly handleSkyLightingChange: (event: Event) => void = (event: Event): void => {
        const skyLightingValue: string = this.nodes.skyLighting.value;

        if (!isSkylightChoice(skyLightingValue)) {
            throw new Error(`Invalid sky-lighting value: ${skyLightingValue}`);
        }

        const skyLighting: SkylightChoice = skyLightingValue;

        this.actions.onConfigChange({
            skyLighting,
        });

        this.logConfigChange(event);
    };

    private readonly handleAutoLaunchChange: (event: Event) => void = (event: Event): void => {
        this.actions.onConfigChange({
            autoLaunch: this.nodes.autoLaunch.checked,
        });
        this.logConfigChange(event);
    };

    private readonly handleFinaleChange: (event: Event) => void = (event: Event): void => {
        this.actions.onConfigChange({
            finale: this.nodes.finaleMode.checked,
        });
        this.logConfigChange(event);
    };

    private readonly handleHideControlsChange: (event: Event) => void = (event: Event): void => {
        this.actions.onConfigChange({
            hideControls: this.nodes.hideControls.checked,
        });
        this.logConfigChange(event);
    };

    private readonly handleLongExposureChange: (event: Event) => void = (event: Event): void => {
        this.actions.onConfigChange({
            longExposure: this.nodes.longExposure.checked,
        });
        this.logConfigChange(event);
    };

    private readonly handleFullscreenChange: (event: Event) => void = (event: Event): void => {
        this.actions.onToggleFullscreen();
        this.logConfigChange(event);
    };

    private readonly handleScaleFactorChange: (event: Event) => void = (event: Event): void => {
        const scaleFactor: number = Number.parseFloat(this.nodes.scaleFactor.value);

        if (Number.isNaN(scaleFactor)) {
            throw new Error(`Invalid scale factor: ${this.nodes.scaleFactor.value}`);
        }

        this.actions.onConfigChange({
            scaleFactor,
        });

        this.actions.onScaleFactorChange();
        this.logConfigChange(event);
    };

    private readonly handlePointerEvent: (event: Event) => void = (event: Event): void => {
        event.stopPropagation();
    };

    public constructor(nodes: AppNodes, store: AppStore, options: SettingsMenuOptions, actions: SettingsMenuActions) {
        super(nodes.menu);

        this.nodes = nodes;
        this.store = store;
        this.options = options;
        this.actions = actions;
    }

    public mount(): void {
        this.populateSelect(this.nodes.shellType, this.options.shellNameOptions);

        this.populateSelect(this.nodes.shellSize, this.options.shellSizeOptions);

        this.populateSelect(this.nodes.quality, this.options.qualityOptions);

        this.populateSelect(this.nodes.skyLighting, this.options.skyLightingOptions);

        this.populateSelect(this.nodes.scaleFactor, this.options.scaleFactorOptions);

        if (!this.options.fullscreenSupported) {
            this.nodes.fullscreenFormOption.classList.add('remove');
        }

        this.nodes.closeMenuBtn.addEventListener('click', this.handleCloseClick);

        this.nodes.shellType.addEventListener('change', this.handleShellTypeChange);

        this.nodes.shellSize.addEventListener('change', this.handleShellSizeChange);

        this.nodes.quality.addEventListener('change', this.handleQualityChange);

        this.nodes.skyLighting.addEventListener('change', this.handleSkyLightingChange);

        this.nodes.autoLaunch.addEventListener('change', this.handleAutoLaunchChange);

        this.nodes.finaleMode.addEventListener('change', this.handleFinaleChange);

        this.nodes.hideControls.addEventListener('change', this.handleHideControlsChange);

        this.nodes.longExposure.addEventListener('change', this.handleLongExposureChange);

        this.nodes.fullscreen.addEventListener('change', this.handleFullscreenChange);

        this.nodes.scaleFactor.addEventListener('change', this.handleScaleFactorChange);

        this.mountHelpListeners();

        for (const eventType of this.blockedPointerEventTypes) {
            this.element.addEventListener(eventType, this.handlePointerEvent, true);
        }

        this.unsubscribe = this.store.subscribe((): void => {
            this.render();
        });

        this.render();
    }

    public render(): void {
        const state: Readonly<AppState> = this.store.state;

        this.element.classList.toggle('hide', !state.menuOpen);

        this.nodes.menuInnerWrap.style.opacity = state.openHelpTopic ? '0.12' : '1';

        this.nodes.shellType.value = state.config.shell;

        this.nodes.shellSize.value = state.config.size;

        this.nodes.quality.value = state.config.quality;

        this.nodes.skyLighting.value = state.config.skyLighting;

        this.nodes.scaleFactor.value = state.config.scaleFactor.toFixed(2);

        this.nodes.autoLaunch.checked = state.config.autoLaunch;

        this.nodes.finaleMode.checked = state.config.finale;

        this.nodes.hideControls.checked = state.config.hideControls;

        this.nodes.longExposure.checked = state.config.longExposure;

        this.nodes.fullscreen.checked = state.fullscreen;

        /*
         * Preserve existing behavior exactly.
         * This can be revisited when Finale Mode UI logic is refined.
         */
        this.nodes.finaleModeFormOption.style.opacity = '1';
    }

    public override destroy(): void {
        this.nodes.closeMenuBtn.removeEventListener('click', this.handleCloseClick);

        this.nodes.shellType.removeEventListener('change', this.handleShellTypeChange);

        this.nodes.shellSize.removeEventListener('change', this.handleShellSizeChange);

        this.nodes.quality.removeEventListener('change', this.handleQualityChange);

        this.nodes.skyLighting.removeEventListener('change', this.handleSkyLightingChange);

        this.nodes.autoLaunch.removeEventListener('change', this.handleAutoLaunchChange);

        this.nodes.finaleMode.removeEventListener('change', this.handleFinaleChange);

        this.nodes.hideControls.removeEventListener('change', this.handleHideControlsChange);

        this.nodes.longExposure.removeEventListener('change', this.handleLongExposureChange);

        this.nodes.fullscreen.removeEventListener('change', this.handleFullscreenChange);

        this.nodes.scaleFactor.removeEventListener('change', this.handleScaleFactorChange);

        for (const [helpNode, handler] of this.helpHandlers) {
            helpNode.removeEventListener('click', handler);
        }

        this.helpHandlers.clear();

        for (const eventType of this.blockedPointerEventTypes) {
            this.element.removeEventListener(eventType, this.handlePointerEvent, true);
        }

        this.unsubscribe?.();
        this.unsubscribe = undefined;
    }

    private populateSelect(node: HTMLSelectElement, options: readonly SelectOption[]): void {
        const documentRef: Document = this.element.ownerDocument;

        node.replaceChildren(
            ...options.map((option: SelectOption): HTMLOptionElement => {
                const optionElement: HTMLOptionElement = documentRef.createElement('option');

                optionElement.value = String(option.value);

                optionElement.textContent = option.label;

                return optionElement;
            }),
        );
    }

    private mountHelpListeners(): void {
        for (const [nodeKey, helpTopic] of Object.entries(this.options.helpNodeToTopic) as Array<
            [HelpNodeKey, HelpTopicKey]
        >) {
            const helpNode: HTMLLabelElement = this.nodes.getHelpNode(nodeKey);

            const handler: (event: MouseEvent) => void = (event: MouseEvent): void => {
                /*
                 * Prevent the default label behavior.
                 *
                 * Checkbox labels use `for="checkbox-id"`, which would otherwise
                 * toggle the associated checkbox when the help label is clicked.
                 */
                event.preventDefault();

                /*
                 * Prevent this interaction from reaching other UI or stage handlers.
                 */
                event.stopPropagation();

                this.actions.onOpenHelp(helpTopic);
            };

            this.helpHandlers.set(helpNode, handler);

            helpNode.addEventListener('click', handler);
        }
    }

    private logConfigChange(event: Event): void {
        const node: EventTarget | null = event.currentTarget;

        if (node instanceof HTMLSelectElement) {
            const output: SettingsChangeConsoleOutput = {
                kind: 'select',
                className: node.className,
                value: node.value,
            };

            console.log('[SELECT CHANGE]', output);
            //this.store.logPersistedStore();
            return;
        }

        if (node instanceof HTMLInputElement) {
            const output: SettingsChangeConsoleOutput = {
                kind: 'input',
                className: node.className,
                checked: node.checked,
            };

            console.log('[INPUT CHANGE]', output);
            //this.store.logPersistedStore();
            return;
        }
    }
}

//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-<  END  >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE NAME: : SettingsMenu.ts

// PROCESSED: 2026-10-09T19:04:33.965Z

//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE NAME: : App.ts 
// TYPE     : .TS
// PATH     : src/app/App.ts
// PROCESSED: 2026-10-09T19:04:33.965Z
//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE-PATH: src/app/App.ts

import type { AppController } from './AppController';
import type { AppStore } from './AppStore';
import type { AppUiConfiguration } from './AppUiConfiguration';
import type { Audio } from '../services/Audio';
import type { AppNodes } from '../dom/AppNodes';
import type { FireworksRuntime } from '../engine/FireworksRuntime';
import type { FullscreenController } from '../services/FullScreen';

import { AppInitializer } from './AppInitializer';
import { AppStateEffects } from './AppStateEffects';
import { AppUi } from './AppUi';
import { AppUiActionFactory } from './AppUiActionFactory';
import { StartupController } from './StartupController';
import type { PerformancePane } from '../debug/PerformancePane';

export interface AppOptions {
    readonly nodes: AppNodes;
    readonly store: AppStore;
    readonly appController: AppController;
    readonly audio: Audio;
    readonly fullscreenController: FullscreenController;
    readonly runtime: FireworksRuntime;
    readonly uiConfiguration: AppUiConfiguration;
    readonly isHeaderMode: boolean;
    readonly performancePane: PerformancePane;
    removeLoadingState(): void;
}

export class App {
    private readonly appUiActionFactory: AppUiActionFactory;
    private readonly appUi: AppUi;
    private readonly appStateEffects: AppStateEffects;
    private readonly appInitializer: AppInitializer;
    private readonly performancePane: PerformancePane;
    private readonly startupController: StartupController;
    private readonly options: AppOptions;

    private started: boolean = false;

    public constructor(options: AppOptions) {
        this.options = options;

        this.performancePane = this.options.performancePane;

        this.appUiActionFactory = new AppUiActionFactory({
            store: this.options.store,
            appController: this.options.appController,
            runtime: this.options.runtime,
            fullscreenController: this.options.fullscreenController,
        });

        this.appUi = new AppUi({
            nodes: this.options.nodes,
            store: this.options.store,

            configuration: this.options.uiConfiguration,
            actions: this.appUiActionFactory.create(),
        });

        this.appStateEffects = new AppStateEffects({
            store: this.options.store,
            appController: this.options.appController,
            audio: this.options.audio,
            nodes: this.options.nodes,
        });

        this.appInitializer = new AppInitializer({
            nodes: this.options.nodes,
            appController: this.options.appController,
            appStateEffects: this.appStateEffects,
            appUi: this.appUi,
            runtime: this.options.runtime,

            removeLoadingState: (): void => {
                this.options.removeLoadingState();
            },

            applyInitialConfiguration: (): void => {
                this.options.appController.initialize();
            },
        });

        this.startupController = new StartupController({
            audio: this.options.audio,
            isHeaderMode: this.options.isHeaderMode,

            initialize: (): void => {
                this.appInitializer.initialize();
            },

            setLoadingStatus: (status: string): void => {
                const loadingStatusNode: Element | null = document.querySelector('.loading-init__status');

                if (loadingStatusNode !== null) {
                    loadingStatusNode.textContent = status;
                }
            },
        });
    }

    public start(): void {
        if (this.started) return;

        this.started = true;

        this.options.fullscreenController.mount();
        this.startupController.start();
    }

    public destroy(): void {
        if (!this.started) return;

        this.started = false;

        this.options.fullscreenController.destroy();
        this.appStateEffects.destroy();
        this.appUi.destroy();
        this.performancePane.destroy();
        this.options.runtime.destroy();
    }
}

//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-<  END  >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■

// FILE NAME: : AppController.ts 
// TYPE     : .TS
// PATH     : src/app/AppController.ts
// PROCESSED: 2026-10-09T19:04:33.965Z
//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE-PATH: src/app/AppController.ts

import type { AppConfig, AppState } from './AppState';
import type { AppStore } from './AppStore';
import type { SparkManager } from '../engine/particles/Spark';
import {
    type QualityLevel,
    type SkylightLevel,
    QUALITY,
    SKYLIGHT,
    toQualityLevel,
    toSkylightLevel,
} from '../constants/config';
import { COLOR_BLACK } from '../constants/colors';

export interface AppControllerOptions {
    readonly store: AppStore;
    readonly sparks: SparkManager;
    readonly canvasContainer: HTMLElement;
}

export class AppController {
    private readonly options: AppControllerOptions;

    private quality: QualityLevel = toQualityLevel(QUALITY.NORMAL);
    private lowQuality: boolean = false;
    private normalQuality: boolean = true;
    private highQuality: boolean = false;

    public constructor(options: AppControllerOptions) {
        this.options = options;
    }

    public initialize(): void {
        this.applyConfig();
    }

    public togglePause(toggle?: boolean): void {
        const paused: boolean = this.options.store.state.paused;
        const nextPaused: boolean = toggle ?? !paused;

        if (paused === nextPaused) {
            return;
        }

        this.options.store.set({
            paused: nextPaused,
        });
    }

    public toggleSound(toggle?: boolean): void {
        const soundEnabled: boolean = this.options.store.state.soundEnabled;
        const nextSoundEnabled: boolean = toggle ?? !soundEnabled;

        if (soundEnabled === nextSoundEnabled) {
            return;
        }

        this.options.store.set({
            soundEnabled: nextSoundEnabled,
        });
    }

    public toggleMenu(toggle?: boolean): void {
        const menuOpen: boolean = this.options.store.state.menuOpen;
        const nextMenuOpen: boolean = toggle ?? !menuOpen;

        if (menuOpen === nextMenuOpen) {
            return;
        }

        this.options.store.set({
            menuOpen: nextMenuOpen,
        });
    }

    public updateConfig(nextConfig?: Partial<AppConfig>): void {
        if (nextConfig !== undefined) this.options.store.setConfig(nextConfig);
        this.applyConfig();
    }

    public isRunning(state: Readonly<AppState> = this.options.store.state): boolean {
        return !state.paused && !state.menuOpen;
    }

    public isSoundEnabled(state: Readonly<AppState> = this.options.store.state): boolean {
        return state.soundEnabled;
    }

    public canPlaySound(state: Readonly<AppState> = this.options.store.state): boolean {
        return this.isRunning(state) && this.isSoundEnabled(state);
    }

    public getQuality(): QualityLevel {
        return this.quality;
    }

    public get isLowQuality(): boolean {
        return this.lowQuality;
    }

    public get isNormalQuality(): boolean {
        return this.normalQuality;
    }

    public get isHighQuality(): boolean {
        return this.highQuality;
    }

    public getConfiguredShellName(): string {
        return this.options.store.state.config.shell;
    }

    public getSelectedShellSize(): number {
        return Number(this.options.store.state.config.size);
    }

    public isFinaleEnabled(): boolean {
        return this.options.store.state.config.finale;
    }

    public isAutoLaunchEnabled(): boolean {
        return this.options.store.state.config.autoLaunch;
    }

    public isLongExposureEnabled(): boolean {
        return this.options.store.state.config.longExposure;
    }

    public getSkyLightingLevel(): SkylightLevel {
        return toSkylightLevel(this.options.store.state.config.skyLighting);
    }

    public getScaleFactor(): number {
        return this.options.store.state.config.scaleFactor;
    }

    private applyConfig(): void {
        const config: Readonly<AppConfig> = this.options.store.state.config;
        const quality: QualityLevel = toQualityLevel(config.quality);

        this.quality = quality;
        this.lowQuality = quality === toQualityLevel(QUALITY.LOW);
        this.normalQuality = quality === toQualityLevel(QUALITY.NORMAL);
        this.highQuality = quality === toQualityLevel(QUALITY.HIGH);

        if (config.skyLighting === SKYLIGHT.NONE) {
            this.options.canvasContainer.style.backgroundColor = COLOR_BLACK;
        }

        this.options.sparks.drawWidth = this.highQuality ? 0.75 : 1;
    }
}

// FILE-PATH: src/app/AppInitializer.ts

import type { AppController } from './AppController';
import type { AppStateEffects } from './AppStateEffects';
import type { AppUi } from './AppUi';
import type { AppNodes } from '../dom/AppNodes';
import type { FireworksRuntime } from '../engine/FireworksRuntime';

export interface AppInitializerOptions {
    readonly nodes: AppNodes;
    readonly appController: AppController;
    readonly appStateEffects: AppStateEffects;
    readonly appUi: AppUi;
    readonly runtime: FireworksRuntime;

    removeLoadingState(): void;
    applyInitialConfiguration(): void;
}

export class AppInitializer {
    private readonly options: AppInitializerOptions;

    public constructor(options: AppInitializerOptions) {
        this.options = options;
    }

    public initialize(): void {
        this.options.removeLoadingState();
        this.options.nodes.stageContainer.classList.remove('remove');

        this.options.appController.togglePause(false);

        this.options.appUi.mount();
        this.options.appStateEffects.mount();

        this.options.runtime.initialize();

        this.options.applyInitialConfiguration();
    }
}

// FILE-PATH: src/app/AppState.ts

import { type QualityChoice, type SkylightChoice, QUALITY, SKYLIGHT } from '../constants/config';

export type HelpTopicKey =
    | 'shellType'
    | 'shellSize'
    | 'quality'
    | 'skyLighting'
    | 'scaleFactor'
    | 'autoLaunch'
    | 'finaleMode'
    | 'hideControls'
    | 'fullscreen'
    | 'longExposure';

export interface AppConfig {
    /**
     * A shell name, such as "Random", "Crysanthemum", or "Willow".
     * This will become a stricter union after shell factories are extracted.
     */
    shell: string;

    /**
     * Stored as a string because it is used directly by a <select> element.
     */
    size: string;

    /**
     * Stored as a string because it is used directly by <select> elements.
     * Convert with Number(...) or unary + when numeric behavior is required.
     */
    quality: QualityChoice;
    /**
     * Stored as a string because it is used directly by a <select> element.
     */
    skyLighting: SkylightChoice;

    scaleFactor: number;
    autoLaunch: boolean;
    finale: boolean;
    hideControls: boolean;
    longExposure: boolean;
}

export interface AppState {
    paused: boolean;
    soundEnabled: boolean;
    menuOpen: boolean;
    openHelpTopic: HelpTopicKey | null;
    fullscreen: boolean;
    config: AppConfig;
}

export interface InitialOptions {
    isDesktop: boolean;
    isHeader: boolean;
    isHighEndDevice: boolean;
    fullscreen: boolean;
    defaultScaleFactor: number;
}

type InitialStateFactory = (options: InitialOptions) => AppState;

export const createInitialState: InitialStateFactory = (options: InitialOptions): AppState => {
    return {
        paused: true,
        soundEnabled: false,
        menuOpen: false,
        openHelpTopic: null,
        fullscreen: options.fullscreen,

        config: {
            shell: 'Random',

            /*
             * This intentionally preserves the current application's behavior.
             *
             * Note: isHeader is derived from isDesktop, so the original ordering
             * means the "1.2" branch is currently unreachable:
             *
             * IS_DESKTOP ? "3" : IS_HEADER ? "1.2" : "2"
             *
             * Do not fix that behavior during this mechanical migration unless
             * changing the header default is an intentional requirement.
             */
            size: options.isDesktop ? '3' : options.isHeader ? '1.2' : '2',

            quality: options.isHighEndDevice ? QUALITY.HIGH : QUALITY.NORMAL,
            skyLighting: SKYLIGHT.NORMAL,
            scaleFactor: options.defaultScaleFactor,

            autoLaunch: true,
            finale: false,
            hideControls: options.isHeader,
            longExposure: false,
        },
    };
};

// PROCESSED: 2026-10-09T19:04:33.965Z
//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE-PATH: src/app/AppStateEffects.ts

import type { AppState } from './AppState';
import type { AppStore } from './AppStore';
import type { Audio } from '../services/Audio';
import type { AppController } from './AppController';
import type { AppNodes } from '../dom/AppNodes';

export interface AppStateEffectsOptions {
    readonly store: AppStore;
    readonly appController: AppController;
    readonly audio: Audio;
    readonly nodes: AppNodes;
}

export class AppStateEffects {
    private readonly options: AppStateEffectsOptions;

    private unsubscribeRender: (() => void) | undefined;
    private unsubscribeStateChange: (() => void) | undefined;

    public constructor(options: AppStateEffectsOptions) {
        this.options = options;
    }

    public mount(): void {
        this.unsubscribeRender = this.options.store.subscribe(
            (state: AppState): void => {
                this.render(state);
            },
        );

        this.unsubscribeStateChange = this.options.store.subscribe(
            (state: AppState, previousState: AppState): void => {
                this.handleStateChange(state, previousState);
            },
        );

        this.render(this.options.store.state);
    }

    public destroy(): void {
        this.unsubscribeRender?.();
        this.unsubscribeRender = undefined;

        this.unsubscribeStateChange?.();
        this.unsubscribeStateChange = undefined;
    }

    private render(state: AppState): void {
        this.options.nodes.canvasContainer.classList.toggle(
            'blur',
            state.menuOpen,
        );

        this.options.nodes.finaleModeFormOption.style.opacity = '1';
    }

    private handleStateChange(
        state: AppState,
        previousState: AppState,
    ): void {
        const canPlaySound: boolean = this.options.appController.canPlaySound(state);
        const couldPlaySound: boolean = this.options.appController.canPlaySound(previousState);

        if (canPlaySound === couldPlaySound) {
            return;
        }

        if (canPlaySound) {
            this.options.audio.resumeAll();
            return;
        }

        this.options.audio.pauseAll();
    }
}
//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-<  END  >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■

//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE NAME: : AppStore.ts 
// TYPE     : .TS
// PATH     : src/app/AppStore.ts
// PROCESSED: 2026-10-09T19:04:33.965Z
//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE-PATH: src/app/AppStore.ts

import type { AppConfig, AppState } from './AppState.js';
import { isQualityChoice, isSkylightChoice } from '../constants/config';

const STORAGE_KEY: string = 'cm_fireworks_data';
const LEGACY_SCHEMA_VERSION_KEY: string = 'schemaVersion';
const LEGACY_CONFIG_SIZE_KEY: string = 'configSize';

interface PersistedConfigV11 {
    quality: string | number;
    size: string;
    skyLighting: string | number;
}

interface PersistedConfigV12 extends PersistedConfigV11 {
    scaleFactor: number;
}

interface PersistedConfigV2 extends PersistedConfigV12 {
    shell: string;
    autoLaunch: boolean;
    finale: boolean;
    hideControls: boolean;
    longExposure: boolean;
}

interface PersistedStoreV11 {
    schemaVersion: '1.1';
    data: PersistedConfigV11;
}

interface PersistedStoreV12 {
    schemaVersion: '1.2';
    data: PersistedConfigV12;
}

interface PersistedStoreV2 {
    schemaVersion: '2.0';
    data: PersistedConfigV2;
}

type PersistedStore = PersistedStoreV11 | PersistedStoreV12 | PersistedStoreV2;

export type StateListener = (state: Readonly<AppState>, previousState: Readonly<AppState>) => void;

export class AppStore {
    private readonly listeners = new Set<StateListener>();
    private currentState: AppState;
    private readonly storage: Storage;

    public constructor(currentState: AppState, storage?: Storage) {
        this.currentState = currentState;
        this.storage = storage ?? window.localStorage;
    }

    /**
     * Compatibility getter for the existing codebase.
     *
     * Existing main.ts references such as store.state.paused can remain in place
     * during this migration.
     */
    public get state(): Readonly<AppState> {
        return this.currentState;
    }

    public get(): Readonly<AppState> {
        return this.currentState;
    }

    public set(nextState: Partial<AppState>): void {
        const previousState: AppState = this.currentState;

        this.currentState = {
            ...this.currentState,
            ...nextState,
        };

        this.dispatch(previousState);
        this.persist();
    }

    public setConfig(nextConfig: Partial<AppConfig>): void {
        const previousState: AppState = this.currentState;

        this.currentState = {
            ...this.currentState,
            config: {
                ...this.currentState.config,
                ...nextConfig,
            },
        };

        this.dispatch(previousState);
        this.persist();
    }

    public subscribe(listener: StateListener): () => void {
        this.listeners.add(listener);

        return (): void => {
            this.listeners.delete(listener);
        };
    }

    /**
     * Loads persisted settings. This should run once, before subscriptions.
     */
    public load(): void {
        const serializedData: string | null = this.storage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);

        if (serializedData) {
            const persisted = JSON.parse(serializedData) as PersistedStore;
            const config: AppConfig = this.currentState.config;

            switch (persisted.schemaVersion) {
                case '1.1':
                    const qV11: string = String(persisted.data.quality);
                    const sV11: string = String(persisted.data.skyLighting);
                    if (isQualityChoice(qV11)) config.quality = qV11;
                    if (isSkylightChoice(sV11)) config.skyLighting = sV11;
                    config.size = persisted.data.size;
                    break;

                case '1.2':
                    const qV12: string = String(persisted.data.quality);
                    const sV12: string = String(persisted.data.skyLighting);
                    if (isQualityChoice(qV12)) config.quality = qV12;
                    if (isSkylightChoice(sV12)) config.skyLighting = sV12;
                    config.size = persisted.data.size;
                    config.scaleFactor = persisted.data.scaleFactor;
                    break;

                case '2.0':
                    const qV2: string = String(persisted.data.quality);
                    const sV2: string = String(persisted.data.skyLighting);

                    if (isQualityChoice(qV2)) config.quality = qV2;
                    if (isSkylightChoice(sV2)) config.skyLighting = sV2;

                    config.shell = persisted.data.shell;
                    config.size = persisted.data.size;
                    config.scaleFactor = persisted.data.scaleFactor;
                    config.autoLaunch = persisted.data.autoLaunch;
                    config.finale = persisted.data.finale;
                    config.hideControls = persisted.data.hideControls;
                    config.longExposure = persisted.data.longExposure;
                    break;

                default:
                    throw new Error('Version switch should be exhaustive');
            }

            this.logPersistedStore();
            return;
        }

        this.loadLegacyConfig();
    }

    public logPersistedStore = (): void => {
        const serializedData: string | null = this.storage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);

        if (serializedData) {
            const store = JSON.parse(serializedData) as PersistedStore;
            console.table({
                'FIREWORKS LOADED CONFIGURATION': {
                    'Schema Version': store.schemaVersion,
                    Quality: store.data.quality,
                    Size: store.data.size,
                    'Sky Lighting': store.data.skyLighting,

                    // Optional chaining/nullish coalescing for version-specific fields
                    'Scale Factor': (store.data as PersistedConfigV12).scaleFactor ?? 'N/A',
                    Shell: (store.data as PersistedConfigV2).shell ?? 'N/A',
                    'Auto Launch': (store.data as PersistedConfigV2).autoLaunch ?? 'N/A',
                    Finale: (store.data as PersistedConfigV2).finale ?? 'N/A',
                    'Hide Controls': (store.data as PersistedConfigV2).hideControls ?? 'N/A',
                    'Long Exposure': (store.data as PersistedConfigV2).longExposure ?? 'N/A',
                },
            });
        }
    };

    private loadLegacyConfig(): void {
        if (this.storage.getItem(LEGACY_SCHEMA_VERSION_KEY) !== '1') {
            return;
        }

        let size: unknown;

        try {
            const sizeRaw: string | null = this.storage.getItem(LEGACY_CONFIG_SIZE_KEY);

            size = typeof sizeRaw === 'string' ? JSON.parse(sizeRaw) : undefined;
        } catch (error) {
            console.log('Recovered from error parsing saved config:');
            console.error(error);
            return;
        }

        const sizeInt: number = Number.parseInt(String(size), 10);

        if (sizeInt >= 0 && sizeInt <= 4) {
            this.currentState.config.size = String(sizeInt);
        }
    }

    public persist12(): void {
        const { config } = this.currentState;
        const qualityValue = String(config.quality);
        const skyLightingValue = String(config.skyLighting);
        if (isQualityChoice(qualityValue)) config.quality = qualityValue;
        if (isSkylightChoice(skyLightingValue)) config.skyLighting = skyLightingValue;

        const persisted: PersistedStoreV12 = {
            schemaVersion: '1.2',
            data: {
                quality: config.quality,
                size: config.size,
                skyLighting: config.skyLighting,
                scaleFactor: config.scaleFactor,
            },
        };

        this.storage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(persisted));
    }

    public persist(): void {
        const { config } = this.currentState;

        const persisted: PersistedStoreV2 = {
            schemaVersion: '2.0',
            data: {
                ...config,
                quality: config.quality,
                skyLighting: config.skyLighting,
            },
        };

        this.storage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(persisted));
    }

    private dispatch(previousState: Readonly<AppState>): void {
        for (const listener of this.listeners) {
            listener(this.currentState, previousState);
        }
    }
}

//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-<  END  >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE NAME: : AppStore.ts

// PROCESSED: 2026-10-09T19:04:33.965Z
//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-<  END  >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■

//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE NAME: : AppUi.ts 
// TYPE     : .TS
// PATH     : src/app/AppUi.ts
// PROCESSED: 2026-10-09T19:04:33.965Z
//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE-PATH: src/app/AppUi.ts

import type { AppConfig, HelpTopicKey } from './AppState';
import type { AppUiConfiguration } from './AppUiConfiguration';
import type { AppStore } from './AppStore';
import type { AppNodes } from '../dom/AppNodes';
import { Controls } from '../components/Controls';
import { SettingsMenu } from '../components/SettingsMenu';
import { HelpModal } from '../components/HelpModal';

export interface AppUiActions {
    onTogglePause(): void;
    onToggleSound(): void;
    onToggleMenu(open?: boolean): void;
    onConfigChange(nextConfig: Partial<AppConfig>): void;
    onScaleFactorChange(): void;
    onToggleFullscreen(): void;
    onOpenHelp(topic: HelpTopicKey): void;
    onCloseHelp(): void;
}

export interface AppUiOptions {
    readonly nodes: AppNodes;
    readonly store: AppStore;
    readonly configuration: AppUiConfiguration;
    readonly actions: AppUiActions;
}

export class AppUi {
    private readonly controls: Controls;
    private readonly settingsMenu: SettingsMenu;
    private readonly helpModal: HelpModal;

    private mounted: boolean = false;

    public constructor(options: AppUiOptions) {
        this.controls = new Controls(options.nodes, options.store, {
            onTogglePause: (): void => options.actions.onTogglePause(),
            onToggleSound: (): void => options.actions.onToggleSound(),
            onToggleMenu: (): void => options.actions.onToggleMenu(),
        });

        this.settingsMenu = new SettingsMenu(
            options.nodes,
            options.store,
            {
                shellNameOptions: options.configuration.shellNameOptions,
                shellSizeOptions: options.configuration.shellSizeOptions,
                qualityOptions: options.configuration.qualityOptions,
                skyLightingOptions: options.configuration.skyLightingOptions,
                scaleFactorOptions: options.configuration.scaleFactorOptions,
                helpNodeToTopic: options.configuration.helpNodeToTopic,
                fullscreenSupported: options.configuration.fullscreenSupported,
            },
            {
                onClose: (): void => options.actions.onToggleMenu(false),
                onConfigChange: (nextConfig: Partial<AppConfig>): void => options.actions.onConfigChange(nextConfig),
                onScaleFactorChange: (): void => options.actions.onScaleFactorChange(),
                onToggleFullscreen: (): void => options.actions.onToggleFullscreen(),
                onOpenHelp: (topic: HelpTopicKey): void => options.actions.onOpenHelp(topic),
            },
        );

        this.helpModal = new HelpModal(options.nodes, options.store, {
            onClose: (): void => options.actions.onCloseHelp(),
        });
    }

    public mount(): void {
        if (this.mounted) return;
        this.mounted = true;
        this.controls.mount();
        this.settingsMenu.mount();
        this.helpModal.mount();
    }

    public destroy(): void {
        if (!this.mounted) return;
        this.mounted = false;
        this.controls.destroy();
        this.settingsMenu.destroy();
        this.helpModal.destroy();
    }
}

//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-<  END  >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■

//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE NAME: : AppUiActionFactory.ts 
// TYPE     : .TS
// PATH     : src/app/AppUiActionFactory.ts
// PROCESSED: 2026-10-09T19:04:33.965Z
//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-< START >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
// FILE-PATH: src/app/AppUiActionFactory.ts

import type { AppConfig, HelpTopicKey } from './AppState';
import type { AppStore } from './AppStore';
import type { AppController } from './AppController';
import type { AppUiActions } from './AppUi';
import type { FireworksRuntime } from '../engine/FireworksRuntime';
import type { FullscreenController } from '../services/FullScreen';

export interface AppUiActionFactoryOptions {
    readonly store: AppStore;
    readonly appController: AppController;
    readonly runtime: FireworksRuntime;
    readonly fullscreenController: FullscreenController;
}

export class AppUiActionFactory {
    private readonly options: AppUiActionFactoryOptions;

    public constructor(options: AppUiActionFactoryOptions) {
        this.options = options;
    }

    public create(): AppUiActions {
        return {
            onTogglePause: (): void => {
                this.options.appController.togglePause();
            },

            onToggleSound: (): void => {
                this.options.appController.toggleSound();
            },

            onToggleMenu: (open?: boolean): void => {
                this.options.appController.toggleMenu(open);
            },

            onConfigChange: (nextConfig: Partial<AppConfig>): void => {
                this.options.appController.updateConfig(nextConfig);
            },

            onScaleFactorChange: (): void => {
                this.options.runtime.resize();
            },

            onToggleFullscreen: (): void => {
                void this.options.fullscreenController.toggle();
            },

            onOpenHelp: (topic: HelpTopicKey): void => {
                this.options.store.set({
                    openHelpTopic: topic,
                });
            },

            onCloseHelp: (): void => {
                this.options.store.set({
                    openHelpTopic: null,
                });
            },
        };
    }
}

//■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■-<  END  >-■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■
